    1: import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { JournalEntry } from './accounting.schema';
    5: import { FinanceGlAdapter } from './finance-gl-adapter.service';
    6: import { FinanceTransaction } from '../finance/finance.schema';
    7: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    8: import { organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
    9: 
   10: export type BackfillReport = {
   11:   organizationId: string;
   12:   scanned: number;
   13:   alreadyPosted: number;
   14:   newlyPosted: number;
   15:   skipped: number;
   16:   errors: Array<{ transactionId: string; transactionDocId: string; error: string }>;
   17:   durationMs: number;
   18: };
   19: 
   20: @Injectable()
   21: export class BackfillService {
   22:   private readonly logger = new Logger(BackfillService.name);
   23: 
   24:   constructor(
   25:     @InjectModel(FinanceTransaction.name) private transactions: Model<FinanceTransaction>,
   26:     @InjectModel(JournalEntry.name) private entries: Model<JournalEntry>,
   27:     private readonly gl: FinanceGlAdapter,
   28:   ) {}
   29: 
   30:   async backfill(rawContext: OrganizationContext): Promise<BackfillReport> {
   31:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   32:     const start = Date.now();
   33: 
   34:     const txs = await this.transactions
   35:       .find(organizationFilter(context))
   36:       .sort({ date: 1 })
   37:       .lean();
   38: 
   39:     const report: BackfillReport = {
   40:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   41:       scanned: txs.length,
   42:       alreadyPosted: 0,
   43:       newlyPosted: 0,
   44:       skipped: 0,
   45:       errors: [],
   46:       durationMs: 0,
   47:     };
   48: 
   49:     for (const tx of txs) {
   50:       // Check for existing JournalEntry
   51:       const existing = await this.entries.findOne(
   52:         organizationFilter(context, {
   53:           sourceType: 'FinanceTransaction',
   54:           sourceId: String(tx._id),
   55:         }),
   56:       ).lean();
   57:       if (existing) {
   58:         report.alreadyPosted++;
   59:         continue;
   60:       }
   61: 
   62:       // Skip Transfers (no journal = intentional skip from Phase A)
   63:       if (tx.direction === 'Transfer') {
   64:         report.skipped++;
   65:         continue;
   66:       }
   67: 
   68:       try {
   69:         await this.gl.postTransaction(context, tx);
   70:         report.newlyPosted++;
   71:       } catch (e: any) {
   72:         const error = e?.message ?? String(e);
   73:         this.logger.error(`Backfill failed for ${tx.transactionId}: ${error}`);
   74:         report.errors.push({
   75:           transactionId: tx.transactionId,
   76:           transactionDocId: String(tx._id),
   77:           error,
   78:         });
   79:       }
   80:     }
   81: 
   82:     report.durationMs = Date.now() - start;
   83:     this.logger.log(
   84:       `Backfill complete: scanned=${report.scanned} newlyPosted=${report.newlyPosted} ` +
   85:       `already=${report.alreadyPosted} skipped=${report.skipped} errors=${report.errors.length} ` +
   86:       `in ${report.durationMs}ms`,
   87:     );
   88:     return report;
   89:   }
   90: }

    1: import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { ChartOfAccount, JournalEntry } from './accounting.schema';
    5: import { PostingService } from './posting.service';
    6: import { DEFAULT_CHART } from './default-chart';
    7: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    8: import { organizationDocument, organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
    9: import { FinanceTransaction } from '../finance/finance.schema';
   10: import {
   11:   cashAccountFor,
   12:   expenseAccountFor,
   13:   incomeAccountFor,
   14: } from './category-map';
   15: 
   16: @Injectable()
   17: export class FinanceGlAdapter {
   18:   private readonly logger = new Logger(FinanceGlAdapter.name);
   19: 
   20:   constructor(
   21:     private readonly posting: PostingService,
   22:     @InjectModel(ChartOfAccount.name) private accounts: Model<ChartOfAccount>,
   23:     @InjectModel(JournalEntry.name) private journalEntries: Model<JournalEntry>,
   24:   ) {}
   25: 
   26:   /** Post a finance transaction to the GL. Safe to call multiple times â€” idempotent. */
   27:   async postTransaction(
   28:     context: OrganizationContext,
   29:     txn: FinanceTransaction & { _id?: any },
   30:   ): Promise<void> {
   31:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   32:     await this.ensureChart(ctx);
   33: 
   34:     // Transfers move money between user-recognised buckets without changing total cash.
   35:     // Your current schema has no "destination account" field, so we skip GL posting.
   36:     if (txn.direction === 'Transfer') {
   37:       this.logger.log(`Skipping GL posting for Transfer ${txn.transactionId}`);
   38:       return;
   39:     }
   40: 
   41:     const cashAccount = cashAccountFor(txn.paymentMethod);
   42:     const date = new Date(txn.date);
   43: 
   44:     let lines;
   45:     if (txn.direction === 'Expense') {
   46:       const expenseAccount = expenseAccountFor(txn.category);
   47:       lines = [
   48:         {
   49:           accountCode: expenseAccount,
   50:           debit: txn.amountUgx,
   51:           memo: txn.description,
   52:           businessLine: txn.businessLine,
   53:           counterparty: txn.counterparty,
   54:         },
   55:         {
   56:           accountCode: cashAccount,
   57:           credit: txn.amountUgx,
   58:           memo: txn.description,
   59:           businessLine: txn.businessLine,
   60:         },
   61:       ];
   62:     } else {
   63:       const incomeAccount = incomeAccountFor(txn.category);
   64:       lines = [
   65:         {
   66:           accountCode: cashAccount,
   67:           debit: txn.amountUgx,
   68:           memo: txn.description,
   69:           businessLine: txn.businessLine,
   70:         },
   71:         {
   72:           accountCode: incomeAccount,
   73:           credit: txn.amountUgx,
   74:           memo: txn.description,
   75:           businessLine: txn.businessLine,
   76:           counterparty: txn.counterparty,
   77:         },
   78:       ];
   79:     }
   80: 
   81:     await this.posting.post(ctx, {
   82:       date,
   83:       description: txn.description || txn.category,
   84:       sourceType: 'FinanceTransaction',
   85:       sourceId: String(txn._id),
   86:       createdBy: txn.createdBy,
   87:       lines,
   88:     });
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   /** Check whether a JournalEntry already exists for a given source record. */
   92:   async hasPostedEntry(context: OrganizationContext, sourceId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   93:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   94:     const count = await this.journalEntries.countDocuments(
   95:       organizationFilter(ctx, {
   96:         sourceType: 'FinanceTransaction',
   97:         sourceId,
   98:       }),
   99:     );
  100:     return count > 0;
  101:   }
  102: 
  103:   /** Lazily seed the default chart of accounts for an organization. Idempotent. */
  104:   private async ensureChart(context: OrganizationContext): Promise<void> {
  105:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
  106:     const count = await this.accounts.countDocuments({ organizationId: ctx.organizationId });
  107:     if (count > 0) return;
  108:     await this.accounts.insertMany(
  109:       DEFAULT_CHART.map(a =>
  110:         organizationDocument(ctx, { ...a, active: true, isSystem: true }),
  111:       ),
  112:     );
  113:     this.logger.log(`Seeded ${DEFAULT_CHART.length} accounts for org ${ctx.organizationId}`);
  114:   }
  115: }

   65:   /** Sum debits/credits per account for posted entries in a date range. */
   66:   private async aggregate(
   67:     context: OrganizationContext,
   68:     dateFilter: { $gte?: Date; $lte?: Date },
   69:     accountTypes?: string[],
   70:   ): Promise<Map<string, { debit: number; credit: number }>> {
   71:     const entryFilter: Record<string, unknown> = {
   72:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   73:       posted: true,
   74:       voided: false,
   75:     };
   76:     if (dateFilter.$gte || dateFilter.$lte) {
   77:       entryFilter.date = dateFilter;
   78:     }
   79:     const entryIds = (await this.entries.find(entryFilter).distinct('_id')) as any[];
   80: 
   81:     const match: Record<string, unknown> = {
   82:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   83:       journalEntryId: { $in: entryIds },
   84:     };
   85: 
   86:     const agg = await this.lines.aggregate([
   87:       { $match: match },
   88:       {
   89:         $group: {
   90:           _id: '$accountCode',
   91:           debit: { $sum: '$debit' },
   92:           credit: { $sum: '$credit' },
   93:         },
   94:       },
   95:     ]);
   96: 
   97:     const result = new Map<string, { debit: number; credit: number }>();
   98:     for (const a of agg) {
   99:       result.set(a._id, { debit: a.debit, credit: a.credit });
  100:     }
  101: 
  102:     // If filtering by account types, only keep those codes
  103:     if (accountTypes && accountTypes.length) {
  104:       const validAccounts = await this.accounts
  105:         .find(organizationFilter(context, { type: { $in: accountTypes } }))
  106:         .lean();
  107:       const validCodes = new Set(validAccounts.map(a => a.code));
  108:       for (const code of Array.from(result.keys())) {
  109:         if (!validCodes.has(code)) result.delete(code);
  110:       }
  111:     }
  112: 
  113:     return result;
  114:   }
  115: 
  116:   /** Resolve account metadata for a set of codes. */
  117:   private async accountMeta(context: OrganizationContext, codes: string[]) {
  118:     const accounts = await this.accounts
  119:       .find(organizationFilter(context, { code: { $in: codes } }))
  120:       .lean();
  121:     return new Map(accounts.map(a => [a.code, a]));
  122:   }
  123: 
  124:   async profitAndLoss(
  125:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  126:     from: Date,
  127:     to: Date,
  128:   ): Promise<ProfitAndLoss> {
  129:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  130:     const agg = await this.aggregate(context, { $gte: from, $lte: to });
  131:     const codes = Array.from(agg.keys());
  132:     const meta = await this.accountMeta(context, codes);
  133: 
  134:     const revenue: ReportLine[] = [];
  135:     const cogs: ReportLine[] = [];
  136:     const expenses: ReportLine[] = [];
  137: 
  138:     for (const [code, { debit, credit }] of agg) {
  139:       const m = meta.get(code);
  140:       if (!m) continue;
  141:       // Revenue: normal credit, amount = credit âˆ’ debit
  142:       if (m.type === 'revenue') {
  143:         const amount = credit - debit;
  144:         if (amount !== 0) {
  145:           revenue.push({ accountCode: code, accountName: m.name, accountType: m.type, amount });
  146:         }
  147:       }
  148:       // COGS: normal debit, amount = debit âˆ’ credit
  149:       else if (m.type === 'cogs') {
  150:         const amount = debit - credit;
  151:         if (amount !== 0) {
  152:           cogs.push({ accountCode: code, accountName: m.name, accountType: m.type, amount });
  153:         }
  154:       }
  155:       // Expense: normal debit
  156:       else if (m.type === 'expense') {
  157:         const amount = debit - credit;
  158:         if (amount !== 0) {
  159:           expenses.push({ accountCode: code, accountName: m.name, accountType: m.type, amount });
  160:         }
  161:       }
  162:     }
  163: 
  164:     revenue.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  165:     cogs.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  166:     expenses.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  167: 
  168:     const revenueTotal = revenue.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  169:     const cogsTotal = cogs.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  170:     const expensesTotal = expenses.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  171:     const grossProfit = revenueTotal - cogsTotal;
  172:     const netIncome = grossProfit - expensesTotal;
  173: 
  174:     return {
  175:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
  176:       from: from.toISOString(),
  177:       to: to.toISOString(),
  178:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  179:       revenue,
  180:       revenueTotal,
  181:       cogs,
  182:       cogsTotal,
  183:       grossProfit,
  184:       expenses,
  185:       expensesTotal,
  186:       netIncome,
  187:     };
  188:   }
  189: 
  190:   async balanceSheet(
  191:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  192:     asOf: Date,
  193:   ): Promise<BalanceSheet> {
  194:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  195: 
  196:     // Cumulative up to asOf
  197:     const agg = await this.aggregate(context, { $lte: asOf });
  198:     const codes = Array.from(agg.keys());
  199:     const meta = await this.accountMeta(context, codes);
  200: 
  201:     const assets: ReportLine[] = [];
  202:     const liabilities: ReportLine[] = [];
  203:     const equity: ReportLine[] = [];
  204: 
  205:     let revenueTotal = 0;
  206:     let cogsTotal = 0;
  207:     let expensesTotal = 0;
  208: 
  209:     for (const [code, { debit, credit }] of agg) {
  210:       const m = meta.get(code);
  211:       if (!m) continue;
  212: 
  213:       if (m.type === 'asset') {
  214:         const amount = debit - credit;
  215:         if (amount !== 0) assets.push({ accountCode: code, accountName: m.name, accountType: m.type, amount });
  216:       } else if (m.type === 'liability') {
  217:         const amount = credit - debit;
  218:         if (amount !== 0) liabilities.push({ accountCode: code, accountName: m.name, accountType: m.type, amount });
  219:       } else if (m.type === 'equity') {
  220:         const amount = credit - debit;
  221:         if (amount !== 0) equity.push({ accountCode: code, accountName: m.name, accountType: m.type, amount });
  222:       } else if (m.type === 'revenue') {
  223:         revenueTotal += credit - debit;
  224:       } else if (m.type === 'cogs') {
  225:         cogsTotal += debit - credit;
  226:       } else if (m.type === 'expense') {
  227:         expensesTotal += debit - credit;
  228:       }
  229:     }
  230: 
  231:     assets.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  232:     liabilities.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  233:     equity.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  234: 
  235:     const retainedEarnings = revenueTotal - cogsTotal - expensesTotal;
  236: 
  237:     const assetsTotal = assets.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  238:     const liabilitiesTotal = liabilities.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  239:     const equityTotal = equity.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0) + retainedEarnings;
  240: 
  241:     const difference = Math.round((assetsTotal - (liabilitiesTotal + equityTotal)) * 100) / 100;
  242: 
  243:     return {
  244:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
  245:       asOf: asOf.toISOString(),
  246:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  247:       assets,
  248:       assetsTotal,
  249:       liabilities,
  250:       liabilitiesTotal,
  251:       equity,
  252:       equityTotal,
  253:       retainedEarnings,
  254:       balanced: Math.abs(difference) < 0.005,
  255:       difference,
  256:     };
  257:   }
  258: 
  259:   async cashFlow(
  260:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  261:     from: Date,
  262:     to: Date,
  263:   ): Promise<CashFlow> {
  264:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  265: 
  266:     // Cash accounts are all asset accounts whose code starts with 10 (1000-1099)
  267:     const cashAccounts = await this.accounts
  268:       .find(organizationFilter(context, {
  269:         type: 'asset',
  270:         code: { $regex: /^10/ },
  271:       }))
  272:       .lean();
  273:     const cashCodes = cashAccounts.map(a => a.code);
  274: 
  275:     // Opening = cumulative up to (from - 1ms)
  276:     const openingDate = new Date(from.getTime() - 1);
  277:     const openingAgg = await this.aggregate(context, { $lte: openingDate });
  278:     let openingCash = 0;
  279:     for (const code of cashCodes) {
  280:       const v = openingAgg.get(code);
  281:       if (v) openingCash += v.debit - v.credit;
  282:     }
  283: 
  284:     // Movement in the period
  285:     const periodAgg = await this.aggregate(context, { $gte: from, $lte: to });
  286:     let closingCash = openingCash;
  287:     for (const code of cashCodes) {
  288:       const v = periodAgg.get(code);
  289:       if (v) closingCash += v.debit - v.credit;
  290:     }
  291: 
  292:     // Detail: journals that touched a cash account in the period
  293:     const entriesInPeriod = await this.entries
  294:       .find(organizationFilter(context, {
  295:         posted: true,
  296:         voided: false,
  297:         date: { $gte: from, $lte: to },
  298:       }))
  299:       .lean();
  300: 
  301:     const entryIds = entriesInPeriod.map(e => e._id);
  302:     const cashLines = await this.lines.find(
  303:       organizationFilter(context, {
  304:         journalEntryId: { $in: entryIds },
  305:         accountCode: { $in: cashCodes },
  306:       }),
  307:     ).lean();
  308: 
  309:     const entryById = new Map(entriesInPeriod.map(e => [String(e._id), e]));
  310:     const inflows: CashFlow['inflows'] = [];
  311:     const outflows: CashFlow['outflows'] = [];
  312: 
  313:     for (const line of cashLines) {
  314:       const entry = entryById.get(String(line.journalEntryId));
  315:       if (!entry) continue;
  316:       const net = line.debit - line.credit;
  317:       if (net === 0) continue;
  318:       const item = {
  319:         date: (entry.date as Date).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  320:         description: entry.description,
  321:         amount: Math.abs(net),
  322:         journalNumber: entry.journalNumber,
  323:       };
  324:       if (net > 0) inflows.push(item);
  325:       else outflows.push(item);
  326:     }
  327:     inflows.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  328:     outflows.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  329: 
  330:     return {
  331:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
  332:       from: from.toISOString(),
  333:       to: to.toISOString(),
  334:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  335:       openingCash,
  336:       closingCash,
  337:       netChange: closingCash - openingCash,
  338:       inflows,
  339:       outflows,
  340:     };
  341:   }
  342: }

   51: export class JournalEntriesService {
   52:   constructor(
   53:     @InjectModel(JournalEntry.name) private entries: Model<JournalEntry>,
   54:     @InjectModel(JournalLine.name) private lines: Model<JournalLine>,
   55:     @InjectModel(ChartOfAccount.name) private accounts: Model<ChartOfAccount>,

   59:   async list(
   60:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   61:     from?: Date,
   62:     to?: Date,
   63:     limit = 200,
   64:   ): Promise<JournalListItem[]> {
   65:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   66:     const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {
   67:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   68:     };
   69:     if (from || to) {
   70:       const dateFilter: Record<string, Date> = {};
   71:       if (from) dateFilter.$gte = from;
   72:       if (to) dateFilter.$lte = to;
   73:       filter.date = dateFilter;
   74:     }
   75: 
   76:     const entries = await this.entries
   77:       .find(filter)
   78:       .sort({ date: -1, createdAt: -1 })
   79:       .limit(limit)
   80:       .lean();
   81: 
   82:     if (entries.length === 0) return [];
   83: 
   84:     // Aggregate totals per entry
   85:     const entryIds = entries.map(e => e._id);
   86:     const sums = await this.lines.aggregate([
   87:       { $match: { organizationId: context.organizationId, journalEntryId: { $in: entryIds } } },
   88:       {
   89:         $group: {
   90:           _id: '$journalEntryId',
   91:           totalDebit: { $sum: '$debit' },
   92:           lineCount: { $sum: 1 },
   93:         },
   94:       },
   95:     ]);
   96:     const totalsById = new Map(sums.map((s: any) => [String(s._id), s]));
   97: 
   98:     return entries.map(e => {
   99:       const totals = totalsById.get(String(e._id));
  100:       return {
  101:         _id: String(e._id),
  102:         journalNumber: e.journalNumber,
  103:         date: (e.date as Date).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  104:         description: e.description,
  105:         sourceType: e.sourceType,
  106:         sourceId: e.sourceId,
  107:         posted: e.posted,
  108:         voided: e.voided ?? false,
  109:         reversalOf: e.reversalOf,
  110:         reversedBy: e.reversedBy,
  111:         createdAt: (e.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
  112:         totalAmount: totals?.totalDebit ?? 0,
  113:         lineCount: totals?.lineCount ?? 0,
  114:       };
  115:     });
  116:   }

  118:   async detail(rawContext: OrganizationContext, id: string): Promise<JournalDetail> {
  119:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  120:     const entry = await this.entries.findOne(
  121:       organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }),
  122:     ).lean();
  123:     if (!entry) throw new NotFoundException('Journal entry not found');
  124: 
  125:     const lines = await this.lines.find(
  126:       organizationFilter(context, { journalEntryId: entry._id }),
  127:     ).lean();
  128: 
  129:     const accountIds = lines.map(l => l.accountId);
  130:     const accounts = await this.accounts.find(
  131:       organizationFilter(context, { _id: { $in: accountIds } }),
  132:     ).lean();
  133:     const accountMap = new Map(accounts.map(a => [String(a._id), a]));
  134: 
  135:     const totalAmount = lines.reduce((s, l) => s + l.debit, 0);
  136: 
  137:     return {
  138:       _id: String(entry._id),
  139:       journalNumber: entry.journalNumber,
  140:       date: (entry.date as Date).toISOString().slice(0, 10),

  142:       sourceType: entry.sourceType,
  143:       sourceId: entry.sourceId,
  144:       posted: entry.posted,

  146:       reversalOf: entry.reversalOf,
  147:       reversedBy: entry.reversedBy,
  148:       createdAt: (entry.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
  149:       totalAmount,
  150:       lineCount: lines.length,
  151:       lines: lines.map(l => {
  152:         const acct = accountMap.get(String(l.accountId));
  153:         return {
  154:           _id: String(l._id),
  155:           accountId: String(l.accountId),
  156:           accountCode: l.accountCode,

  168:   async createManual(
  169:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  170:     input: ManualEntryInput,
  171:     actor: string,
  172:   ): Promise<JournalDetail> {
  173:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  174: 
  175:     if (!input.description || input.description.trim().length < 3) {
  176:       throw new BadRequestException('Description must be at least 3 characters');
  177:     }
  178:     if (!input.lines || input.lines.length < 2) {
  179:       throw new BadRequestException('At least two lines required (one debit, one credit)');
  180:     }
  181: 
  182:     // Validate + normalise lines
  183:     const normalised = input.lines.map((l, i) => {
  184:       const debit = Number(l.debit ?? 0);
  185:       const credit = Number(l.credit ?? 0);
  186:       if (debit < 0 || credit < 0) throw new BadRequestException(`Line ${i + 1}: amounts must be non-negative`);
  187:       if (debit > 0 && credit > 0) throw new BadRequestException(`Line ${i + 1}: cannot have both debit and credit`);
  188:       if (debit === 0 && credit === 0) throw new BadRequestException(`Line ${i + 1}: zero-amount line not allowed`);
  189:       if (!l.accountCode) throw new BadRequestException(`Line ${i + 1}: accountCode required`);
  190:       return {
  191:         accountCode: l.accountCode,
  192:         debit,
  193:         credit,
  194:         memo: l.memo,
  195:       };
  196:     });
  197: 
  198:     const totalDebit = normalised.reduce((s, l) => s + l.debit, 0);
  199:     const totalCredit = normalised.reduce((s, l) => s + l.credit, 0);
  200:     if (Math.abs(totalDebit - totalCredit) > 0.005) {
  201:       throw new BadRequestException(
  202:         `Entry does not balance: debits ${totalDebit} â‰  credits ${totalCredit}`,
  203:       );
  204:     }
  205: 
  206:     // Post via PostingService (sourceType: ManualJournal, sourceId: generated uuid)
  207:     const sourceId = `manual-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9)}`;
  208: 
  209:     const entry = await this.posting.post(context, {
  210:       date: new Date(input.date + 'T12:00:00.000Z'),
  211:       description: input.description.trim(),
  212:       sourceType: 'ManualJournal',
  213:       sourceId,
  214:       createdBy: actor,
  215:       lines: normalised,
  216:     });
  217: 
  218:     return this.detail(rawContext, String(entry._id));
  219:   }
  220: 
  221:   async reverse(
  222:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  223:     id: string,
  224:     reason: string,
  225:     actor: string,
  226:   ): Promise<JournalDetail> {
  227:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  228:     if (!reason || reason.trim().length < 10) {
  229:       throw new BadRequestException('Reversal reason must be at least 10 characters');
  230:     }
  231: 
  232:     const original = await this.entries.findOne(
  233:       organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }),
  234:     ).lean();
  235:     if (!original) throw new NotFoundException('Journal entry not found');
  236:     if (original.voided) throw new BadRequestException('Entry is already voided');
  237:     if (original.reversedBy) {
  238:       throw new BadRequestException(
  239:         `Entry already reversed by ${original.reversedBy}`,
  240:       );
  241:     }
  242:     if (!original.posted) throw new BadRequestException('Cannot reverse an unposted entry');
  243: 
  244:     const originalLines = await this.lines.find(
  245:       organizationFilter(context, { journalEntryId: original._id }),
  246:     ).lean();
  247:     if (originalLines.length === 0) throw new BadRequestException('Original entry has no lines');
  248: 
  249:     // Build reversal lines: swap debit â†” credit
  250:     const reversalLines = originalLines.map(l => ({
  251:       accountCode: l.accountCode,
  252:       debit: l.credit,
  253:       credit: l.debit,
  254:       memo: `Reversal: ${l.memo ?? original.description}`,
  255:       businessLine: l.businessLine,
  256:       counterparty: l.counterparty,
  257:     }));
  258: 
  259:     const sourceId = `${original._id}:reversal:${Date.now()}`;
  260: 
  261:     const reversalEntry = await this.posting.post(context, {
  262:       date: new Date(),
  263:       description: `Reversal of ${original.journalNumber} â€” ${reason.trim()}`,
  264:       sourceType: 'ManualJournal',
  265:       sourceId,
  266:       createdBy: actor,
  267:       lines: reversalLines,
  268:     });
  269: 
  270:     // Link both directions
  271:     await this.entries.updateOne(
  272:       { _id: original._id },
  273:       { $set: { reversedBy: reversalEntry.journalNumber } },
  274:     );
  275:     await this.entries.updateOne(
  276:       { _id: reversalEntry._id },
  277:       { $set: { reversalOf: original.journalNumber } },
  278:     );
  279: 
  280:     return this.detail(rawContext, String(reversalEntry._id));
  281:   }
  282: }

    8: export type PeriodRow = {
    9:   _id: string;
   10:   periodStart: string;
   11:   periodEnd: string;
   12:   status: string;
   13:   locked: boolean;
   14:   lockedAt?: string;

   28:     @InjectModel(JournalEntry.name) private entries: Model<JournalEntry>,

   30: 
   31:   async list(rawContext: OrganizationContext): Promise<PeriodRow[]> {
   32:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   33:     const periods = await this.periods
   34:       .find(organizationFilter(context))
   35:       .sort({ periodStart: -1 })
   36:       .lean();
   37: 
   38:     const result: PeriodRow[] = [];
   39:     for (const p of periods) {
   40:       const [journalEntryCount, postedCount] = await Promise.all([
   41:         this.entries.countDocuments(
   42:           organizationFilter(context, {
   43:             date: { $gte: p.periodStart, $lte: p.periodEnd },
   44:           }),
   45:         ),
   46:         this.entries.countDocuments(
   47:           organizationFilter(context, {
   48:             date: { $gte: p.periodStart, $lte: p.periodEnd },
   49:             posted: true,
   50:             voided: false,
   51:           }),
   52:         ),
   53:       ]);
   54:       result.push({
   55:         _id: String(p._id),
   56:         periodStart: (p.periodStart as Date).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
   57:         periodEnd: (p.periodEnd as Date).toISOString().slice(0, 10),
   58:         status: p.status,
   59:         locked: p.locked,
   60:         lockedAt: p.lockedAt ? (p.lockedAt as Date).toISOString() : undefined,
   61:         lockedBy: p.lockedBy,
   62:         closedAt: p.closedAt ? (p.closedAt as Date).toISOString() : undefined,
   63:         closedBy: p.closedBy,
   64:         reopenReason: p.reopenReason,
   65:         journalEntryCount,
   66:         postedCount,
   67:         unpostedCount: journalEntryCount - postedCount,
   68:       });
   69:     }
   70:     return result;
   71:   }
   72: 
   73:   async lockPeriod(
   74:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   75:     year: number,
   76:     month: number,
   77:     actor: string,
   78:   ): Promise<PeriodRow> {
   79:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   80:     if (month < 1 || month > 12) throw new BadRequestException('month must be 1â€“12');
   81: 
   82:     const start = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, 1));
   83:     const end = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999));
   84: 
   85:     // 1. Find or create the period
   86:     let period = await this.periods.findOne(
   87:       organizationFilter(context, { periodStart: start, periodEnd: end }),
   88:     );
   89: 
   90:     if (!period) {
   91:       period = await this.periods.create(
   92:         organizationDocument(context, {
   93:           periodStart: start,
   94:           periodEnd: end,
   95:           status: 'open',
   96:           locked: false,
   97:         }),
   98:       );
   99:     }
  100: 
  101:     if (period.locked) {
  102:       throw new BadRequestException(
  103:         `Period ${start.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} â€“ ${end.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} is already locked.`,
  104:       );
  105:     }
  106: 
  107:     // 2. Pre-close checks
  108:     const unposted = await this.entries.countDocuments(
  109:       organizationFilter(context, {
  110:         date: { $gte: start, $lte: end },
  111:         posted: false,
  112:         voided: false,
  113:       }),
  114:     );
  115:     if (unposted > 0) {
  116:       throw new BadRequestException(
  117:         `Cannot lock period: ${unposted} unposted journal ${unposted === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries'} in this period. Post them first.`,
  118:       );
  119:     }
  120: 
  121:     // 3. Flip the lock
  122:     period.locked = true;
  123:     period.lockedAt = new Date();
  124:     period.lockedBy = actor;
  125:     period.status = 'closed';
  126:     period.closedAt = new Date();
  127:     period.closedBy = actor;
  128:     await period.save();
  129: 
  130:     return (await this.list(rawContext)).find(p => p._id === String(period!._id))!;
  131:   }
  132: 
  133:   async reopenPeriod(
  134:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  135:     id: string,
  136:     reason: string,
  137:     actor: string,
  138:   ): Promise<PeriodRow> {
  139:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  140:     if (!reason || reason.trim().length < 10) {
  141:       throw new BadRequestException('Reopen reason must be at least 10 characters.');
  142:     }
  143:     const period = await this.periods.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }));
  144:     if (!period) throw new NotFoundException('Period not found');
  145:     if (!period.locked) {
  146:       throw new BadRequestException('Period is already open.');
  147:     }
  148: 
  149:     period.locked = false;
  150:     period.status = 'open';
  151:     period.reopenReason = `${new Date().toISOString()} â€” ${actor}: ${reason.trim()}`;
  152:     // Do not clear lockedAt / closedAt â€” keep audit trail of the original close
  153:     await period.save();
  154: 
  155:     return (await this.list(rawContext)).find(p => p._id === String(period._id))!;
  156:   }

    1: import { BadRequestException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
    4: import {
    5:   AccountingPeriod,
    6:   ChartOfAccount,
    7:   JournalEntry,
    8:   JournalLine,
    9: } from './accounting.schema';
   10: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
   11: import { organizationDocument, organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
   12: 
   13: export type JournalLineInput = {
   14:   accountCode: string;
   15:   debit?: number;
   16:   credit?: number;
   17:   memo?: string;
   18:   businessLine?: string;
   19:   counterparty?: string;
   20: };
   21: 
   22: export type PostEntryInput = {
   23:   date: Date;
   24:   description: string;
   25:   sourceType: string;
   26:   sourceId: string;
   27:   createdBy: string;
   28:   lines: JournalLineInput[];
   29: };
   30: 
   31: @Injectable()
   32: export class PostingService {
   33:   constructor(
   34:     @InjectModel(JournalEntry.name) private entries: Model<JournalEntry>,
   35:     @InjectModel(JournalLine.name) private lines: Model<JournalLine>,
   36:     @InjectModel(ChartOfAccount.name) private accounts: Model<ChartOfAccount>,
   37:     @InjectModel(AccountingPeriod.name) private periods: Model<AccountingPeriod>,
   38:   ) {}
   39: 
   40:   async post(rawContext: OrganizationContext, input: PostEntryInput): Promise<JournalEntry> {
   41:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   42: 
   43:     // 1. Validate: debits === credits, at least 2 lines, all amounts >= 0
   44:     const totals = input.lines.reduce(
   45:       (t, l) => ({
   46:         debit: t.debit + (l.debit ?? 0),
   47:         credit: t.credit + (l.credit ?? 0),
   48:       }),
   49:       { debit: 0, credit: 0 },
   50:     );
   51:     if (input.lines.length < 2) {
   52:       throw new BadRequestException('Journal entry must have at least 2 lines');
   53:     }
   54:     if (Math.abs(totals.debit - totals.credit) > 0.005) {
   55:       throw new BadRequestException(
   56:         `Journal entry does not balance: debits ${totals.debit} â‰  credits ${totals.credit}`,
   57:       );
   58:     }
   59:     for (const l of input.lines) {
   60:       if ((l.debit ?? 0) < 0 || (l.credit ?? 0) < 0) {
   61:         throw new BadRequestException('Debit and credit amounts must be non-negative');
   62:       }
   63:       if ((l.debit ?? 0) > 0 && (l.credit ?? 0) > 0) {
   64:         throw new BadRequestException('A line must have either debit or credit, not both');
   65:       }
   66:     }
   67: 
   68:     // 2. Check period lock
   69:     const period = await this.periods.findOne(
   70:       organizationFilter(context, {
   71:         periodStart: { $lte: input.date },
   72:         periodEnd: { $gte: input.date },
   73:       }),
   74:     ).lean();
   75:     if (period?.locked) {
   76:       throw new BadRequestException(
   77:         `Cannot post to locked period ${period.periodStart.toISOString().slice(0, 10)} â€“ ${period.periodEnd.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}. Reopen the period to post this transaction.`,
   78:       );
   79:     }
   80: 
   81:     // 3. Resolve account codes â†’ account IDs (validates they exist)
   82:     const accountCodes = Array.from(new Set(input.lines.map(l => l.accountCode)));
   83:     const accounts = await this.accounts.find(
   84:       organizationFilter(context, { code: { $in: accountCodes } }),
   85:     ).lean();
   86:     const accountByCode = new Map(accounts.map(a => [a.code, a]));
   87:     const missing = accountCodes.filter(c => !accountByCode.has(c));
   88:     if (missing.length) {
   89:       throw new BadRequestException(
   90:         `Unknown account codes: ${missing.join(', ')}. Create them in the chart of accounts first.`,
   91:       );
   92:     }
   93: 
   94:     // 4. Generate journal number (per org per year)
   95:     const year = input.date.getUTCFullYear();
   96:     const count = await this.entries.countDocuments(
   97:       organizationFilter(context, {
   98:         date: { $gte: new Date(Date.UTC(year, 0, 1)), $lt: new Date(Date.UTC(year + 1, 0, 1)) },
   99:       }),
  100:     );
  101:     const journalNumber = `JE-${year}-${String(count + 1).padStart(5, '0')}`;
  102: 
  103:     // 5. Insert entry (unique index catches duplicates)
  104:     let entry: any;
  105:     try {
  106:       entry = await this.entries.create(
  107:         organizationDocument(context, {
  108:           journalNumber,
  109:           date: input.date,
  110:           description: input.description,
  111:           sourceType: input.sourceType,
  112:           sourceId: input.sourceId,
  113:           createdBy: input.createdBy,
  114:           posted: true,
  115:           postedAt: new Date(),
  116:           postedBy: input.createdBy,
  117:         }),
  118:       );
  119:     } catch (e: any) {
  120:       if (e?.code === 11000) {
  121:         // Already posted for this source â€” return existing
  122:         const existing = await this.entries.findOne(
  123:           organizationFilter(context, {
  124:             sourceType: input.sourceType,
  125:             sourceId: input.sourceId,
  126:           }),
  127:         );
  128:         if (existing) return existing.toObject();
  129:         throw new BadRequestException('Duplicate journal entry');
  130:       }
  131:       throw e;
  132:     }
  133: 
  134:     // 6. Insert lines
  135:     await this.lines.insertMany(
  136:       input.lines.map(l => ({
  137:         organizationId: context.organizationId,
  138:         journalEntryId: entry._id,
  139:         accountId: accountByCode.get(l.accountCode)!._id,
  140:         accountCode: l.accountCode,
  141:         debit: l.debit ?? 0,
  142:         credit: l.credit ?? 0,
  143:         memo: l.memo,
  144:         businessLine: l.businessLine,
  145:         counterparty: l.counterparty,
  146:       })),
  147:     );
  148: 
  149:     return entry.toObject();
  150:   }
  151: 
  152:   /** Get or create the period row for a given date. Used during backfill. */
  153:   async ensurePeriod(context: OrganizationContext, date: Date) {
  154:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
  155:     const start = new Date(Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth(), 1));
  156:     const end = new Date(Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0, 23, 59, 59, 999));
  157:     const existing = await this.periods.findOne(
  158:       organizationFilter(ctx, { periodStart: start, periodEnd: end }),
  159:     );
  160:     if (existing) return existing.toObject();
  161:     const created = await this.periods.create(
  162:       organizationDocument(ctx, { periodStart: start, periodEnd: end, status: 'open', locked: false }),
  163:     );
  164:     return created.toObject();
  165:   }
  166: }

SHA256: 375ABBA2EA5284AFA327879C52E90E275CC11D38F44B554095F372F27664AAD9
TOTAL LINES: 126
    1: import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { ChartOfAccount, JournalEntry, JournalLine } from './accounting.schema';
    5: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    6: import { organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
    7: 
    8: export type TrialBalanceRow = {
    9:   accountId: string;
   10:   accountCode: string;
   11:   accountName: string;
   12:   accountType: string;
   13:   normalSide: 'debit' | 'credit';
   14:   debit: number;
   15:   credit: number;
   16:   net: number;            // signed: debit âˆ’ credit
   17:   netForDisplay: number;  // positive on the account's normal side
   18: };
   19: 
   20: export type TrialBalanceReport = {
   21:   organizationId: string;
   22:   from: string | null;
   23:   to: string | null;
   24:   generatedAt: string;
   25:   rows: TrialBalanceRow[];
   26:   totals: {
   27:     debit: number;
   28:     credit: number;
   29:     difference: number;
   30:   };
   31:   balanced: boolean;
   32: };
   33: 
   34: @Injectable()
   35: export class TrialBalanceService {
   36:   constructor(
   37:     @InjectModel(JournalEntry.name) private entries: Model<JournalEntry>,
   38:     @InjectModel(JournalLine.name) private lines: Model<JournalLine>,
   39:     @InjectModel(ChartOfAccount.name) private accounts: Model<ChartOfAccount>,
   40:   ) {}
   41: 
   42:   async generate(
   43:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   44:     from?: Date,
   45:     to?: Date,
   46:   ): Promise<TrialBalanceReport> {
   47:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   48: 
   49:     // Find all posted journal entries in the date range
   50:     const entryFilter: Record<string, unknown> = {
   51:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   52:       posted: true,
   53:       voided: false,
   54:     };
   55:     if (from || to) {
   56:       const dateFilter: Record<string, Date> = {};
   57:       if (from) dateFilter.$gte = from;
   58:       if (to) dateFilter.$lte = to;
   59:       entryFilter.date = dateFilter;
   60:     }
   61:     const entryIds = (await this.entries.find(entryFilter).distinct('_id')) as any[];
   62: 
   63:     // Aggregate lines by account
   64:     const agg = await this.lines.aggregate([
   65:       { $match: { organizationId: context.organizationId, journalEntryId: { $in: entryIds } } },
   66:       {
   67:         $group: {
   68:           _id: '$accountId',
   69:           accountCode: { $first: '$accountCode' },
   70:           debit: { $sum: '$debit' },
   71:           credit: { $sum: '$credit' },
   72:         },
   73:       },
   74:     ]);
   75: 
   76:     // Fetch account metadata
   77:     const accounts = await this.accounts
   78:       .find(organizationFilter(context))
   79:       .lean();
   80:     const byId = new Map(accounts.map(a => [String(a._id), a]));
   81: 
   82:     // Build rows for every account that has activity; skip zero-activity accounts
   83:     const rows: TrialBalanceRow[] = [];
   84:     let totalDebit = 0;
   85:     let totalCredit = 0;
   86: 
   87:     for (const a of agg) {
   88:       const meta = byId.get(String(a._id));
   89:       if (!meta) continue;
   90:       const net = a.debit - a.credit;
   91:       const netForDisplay = meta.normalSide === 'debit' ? net : -net;
   92:       rows.push({
   93:         accountId: String(a._id),
   94:         accountCode: meta.code,
   95:         accountName: meta.name,
   96:         accountType: meta.type,
   97:         normalSide: meta.normalSide as 'debit' | 'credit',
   98:         debit: a.debit,
   99:         credit: a.credit,
  100:         net,
  101:         netForDisplay,
  102:       });
  103:       totalDebit += a.debit;
  104:       totalCredit += a.credit;
  105:     }
  106: 
  107:     // Sort by account code (which groups assets, liabilities, revenue, expenses in order)
  108:     rows.sort((a, b) => a.accountCode.localeCompare(b.accountCode));
  109: 
  110:     const difference = Math.round((totalDebit - totalCredit) * 100) / 100;
  111: 
  112:     return {
  113:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
  114:       from: from ? from.toISOString() : null,
  115:       to: to ? to.toISOString() : null,
  116:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  117:       rows,
  118:       totals: {
  119:         debit: totalDebit,
  120:         credit: totalCredit,
  121:         difference,
  122:       },
  123:       balanced: Math.abs(difference) < 0.005,
  124:     };
  125:   }
  126: }

   10: const STATUSES = ['Open', 'In progress', 'Blocked', 'Completed', 'Cancelled'];
   11: const PRIORITIES = ['Low', 'Medium', 'High', 'Critical'];
   12: const MUTABLE = ['title', 'description', 'priority', 'status', 'assignedTo', 'dueDate', 'completionNotes'];

   14: @Injectable()
   15: export class ApiaryActionsService {

   17:     @InjectModel(ApiaryAction.name) private readonly actions: Model<ApiaryAction>,

   23:   private async assertHive(context: OrganizationContext, hiveId: string) {
   24:     if (!hiveId || !(await this.hives.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { hiveId })).select('_id').lean())) {
   25:       throw new NotFoundException('Hive not found in the selected organization');
   26:     }
   27:   }
   28: 
   29:   private async event(context: OrganizationContext, actionId: string, actorId: string, eventType: string, changes?: any, note?: string) {
   30:     await this.events.create({ organizationId: context.organizationId, actionId, actorId, eventType, changes, note });
   31:   }
   32: 
   33:   async list(rawContext: OrganizationContext, filters: any = {}) {
   34:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   35:     const query: any = organizationFilter(context);
   36:     if (filters.status) {
   37:       if (!STATUSES.includes(filters.status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid status filter');
   38:       query.status = filters.status;
   39:     }
   40:     if (filters.hiveId) query.hiveId = filters.hiveId;
   41:     if (filters.overdueOnly === 'true') {
   42:       query.dueDate = { $lt: new Date() };
   43:       query.status = { $nin: ['Completed', 'Cancelled'] };
   44:     }
   45:     const limit = Math.min(500, Math.max(1, Number(filters.limit) || 100));
   46:     return this.actions.find(query).sort({ dueDate: 1, createdAt: -1 }).limit(limit).lean();
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   async create(dto: any, rawContext: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {
   50:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   51:     if (!dto?.hiveId || !dto?.title?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('hiveId and title are required');
   52:     await this.assertHive(context, dto.hiveId);
   53:     if (dto.priority && !PRIORITIES.includes(dto.priority)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid priority');
   54:     if (dto.status && !STATUSES.includes(dto.status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid status');
   55:     if (dto.sourceInspectionId) {
   56:       if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(dto.sourceInspectionId)) throw new BadRequestException('sourceInspectionId must be a valid inspection ID');
   57:       const inspection = await this.inspections.findOne(organizationFilter(context, {
   58:         _id: new Types.ObjectId(dto.sourceInspectionId), hiveId: dto.hiveId,
   59:       })).select('_id').lean();
   60:       if (!inspection) throw new NotFoundException('Source inspection not found for this hive and organization');
   61:     }
   62:     if (dto.dueDate && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(dto.dueDate))) throw new BadRequestException('dueDate must be a valid date');
   63:     const actionId = `AA-${new Types.ObjectId().toHexString()}`;
   64:     const payload: any = {
   65:       organizationId: context.organizationId, actionId, hiveId: dto.hiveId,
   66:       sourceInspectionId: dto.sourceInspectionId,
   67:       title: dto.title.trim(), description: dto.description,
   68:       priority: dto.priority || 'Medium', status: dto.status || 'Open',
   69:       assignedTo: dto.assignedTo, dueDate: dto.dueDate ? new Date(dto.dueDate) : undefined,
   70:       completionNotes: dto.completionNotes,
   71:       completedAt: dto.status === 'Completed' ? new Date() : undefined,
   72:       createdBy: actorId, updatedBy: actorId,
   73:     };
   74:     const created = await this.actions.create(payload);
   75:     await this.event(context, actionId, actorId, 'created', { title: payload.title, status: payload.status, priority: payload.priority });
   76:     return created;
   77:   }
   78: 
   79:   async update(id: string, dto: any, rawContext: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {
   80:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   81:     const action = await this.actions.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }));
   82:     if (!action) throw new NotFoundException('Action not found in the selected organization');
   83:     const changes: any = {};
   84:     for (const key of MUTABLE) {
   85:       if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(dto || {}, key)) changes[key] = dto[key];
   86:     }
   87:     if (!Object.keys(changes).length) throw new BadRequestException('No supported action fields supplied');
   88:     if (changes.title !== undefined && (typeof changes.title !== 'string' || !changes.title.trim())) throw new BadRequestException('title cannot be empty');
   89:     if (changes.priority !== undefined && !PRIORITIES.includes(changes.priority)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid priority');
   90:     if (changes.status !== undefined && !STATUSES.includes(changes.status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid status');
   91:     if (changes.dueDate !== undefined) {
   92:       if (changes.dueDate === null || changes.dueDate === '') changes.dueDate = undefined;
   93:       else if (Number.isNaN(Date.parse(changes.dueDate))) throw new BadRequestException('dueDate must be a valid date');
   94:       else changes.dueDate = new Date(changes.dueDate);
   95:     }
   96:     if (changes.title) changes.title = changes.title.trim();
   97:     const before: any = {};
   98:     for (const key of Object.keys(changes)) before[key] = (action as any)[key];
   99:     if (changes.status === 'Completed') changes.completedAt = new Date();
  100:     else if (changes.status && changes.status !== 'Completed') changes.completedAt = undefined;
  101:     changes.updatedBy = actorId;
  102:     Object.assign(action, changes);
  103:     await action.save();
  104:     await this.event(context, action.actionId, actorId, 'updated', { before, after: changes }, dto?.auditNote);
  105:     return action;
  106:   }
  107: 
  108:   async summary(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
  109:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  110:     const rows: any[] = await this.actions.find(organizationFilter(context)).select('status priority dueDate').limit(10000).lean();
  111:     const now = Date.now();
  112:     const result: any = { total: rows.length, overdue: 0, highOrCriticalOpen: 0, byStatus: {} };
  113:     for (const status of STATUSES) result.byStatus[status] = 0;
  114:     for (const row of rows) {
  115:       result.byStatus[row.status] = (result.byStatus[row.status] || 0) + 1;
  116:       const active = !['Completed', 'Cancelled'].includes(row.status);
  117:       if (active && row.dueDate && new Date(row.dueDate).getTime() < now) result.overdue++;
  118:       if (active && ['High', 'Critical'].includes(row.priority)) result.highOrCriticalOpen++;
  119:     }
  120:     return result;
  121:   }

  123:   async history(id: string, rawContext: OrganizationContext) {

  127:     return this.events.find({ organizationId: context.organizationId, actionId: action.actionId })

    1: import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

    8: 
    9: @Injectable()
   10: export class ApiaryIntelligenceService {
   11:   constructor(
   12:     @InjectModel(ApiaryInspection.name) private inspections: Model<ApiaryInspection>,
   13:     @InjectModel(HoneyHarvest.name) private harvests: Model<HoneyHarvest>,
   14:     @InjectModel(Hive.name) private hives: Model<Hive>,
   15:   ) {}
   16: 
   17:   async dashboard(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   18:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   19:     const [hives, inspections, harvests] = await Promise.all([
   20:       this.hives.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ hiveId: 1 }).lean(),
   21:       this.inspections.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ inspectionDate: -1 }).limit(5000).lean(),
   22:       this.harvests.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ harvestDate: -1 }).limit(5000).lean(),
   23:     ]);
   24:     const now = new Date();
   25:     const cutoff = new Date(now);
   26:     cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - 30);
   27:     const recentInspections = inspections.filter((x: any) => new Date(x.inspectionDate) >= cutoff);
   28:     const recentHarvests = harvests.filter((x: any) => new Date(x.harvestDate) >= cutoff);
   29:     const totalHoneyKg = harvests.reduce((sum: number, row: any) => sum + Number(row.quantityKg || 0), 0);
   30:     const healthCounts: Record<string, number> = { Strong: 0, Moderate: 0, Weak: 0, Queenless: 0, Unknown: 0 };
   31:     for (const row of inspections as any[]) {
   32:       const status = healthCounts[row.colonyStrength] === undefined ? 'Unknown' : row.colonyStrength;
   33:       healthCounts[status] += 1;
   34:     }
   35:     const yieldByHive = new Map<string, number>();
   36:     for (const row of harvests as any[]) yieldByHive.set(row.hiveId, (yieldByHive.get(row.hiveId) || 0) + Number(row.quantityKg || 0));
   37:     const productivityRanking = [...yieldByHive.entries()]
   38:       .map(([hiveId, quantityKg]) => ({ hiveId, recordedHoneyKg: Math.round(quantityKg * 100) / 100 }))
   39:       .sort((a, b) => b.recordedHoneyKg - a.recordedHoneyKg);
   40:     const latestInspectionByHive = new Map<string, any>();
   41:     for (const row of inspections as any[]) if (!latestInspectionByHive.has(row.hiveId)) latestInspectionByHive.set(row.hiveId, row);
   42:     const attentionHives = hives.map((hive: any) => {
   43:       const latest = latestInspectionByHive.get(hive.hiveId);
   44:       const reasons: string[] = [];
   45:       if (hive.colonyStatus === 'Weak' || hive.colonyStatus === 'Queenless') reasons.push(`Hive status is ${hive.colonyStatus}`);
   46:       if (!latest) reasons.push('No recorded apiary inspection');
   47:       else {
   48:         if (latest.colonyStrength === 'Weak' || latest.colonyStrength === 'Queenless') reasons.push(`Latest inspection indicates ${latest.colonyStrength.toLowerCase()} colony`);
   49:         if (latest.actionRequired) reasons.push('Inspection has a recorded action required');
   50:         if (latest.pestOrDiseaseLevel === 'High') reasons.push('High pest or disease level recorded');
   51:       }
   52:       return { hiveId: hive.hiveId, apiarySite: hive.apiarySite, latestInspectionDate: latest?.inspectionDate || null, reasons };
   53:     }).filter((x: any) => x.reasons.length > 0);
   54:     return {
   55:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   56:       generatedAt: now.toISOString(),
   57:       overview: {
   58:         registeredHives: hives.length,
   59:         inspectionRecords: inspections.length,
   60:         inspectionsLast30Days: recentInspections.length,
   61:         harvestRecords: harvests.length,
   62:         harvestRecordsLast30Days: recentHarvests.length,
   63:         totalRecordedHoneyKg: Math.round(totalHoneyKg * 100) / 100,
   64:         recordedHoneyKgLast30Days: Math.round(recentHarvests.reduce((sum: number, row: any) => sum + Number(row.quantityKg || 0), 0) * 100) / 100,
   65:       },
   66:       inspectionHealthCounts: healthCounts,
   67:       productivityRanking,
   68:       hivesRequiringAttention: attentionHives,
   69:       limitations: [
   70:         'Insights use recorded data only; missing inspections do not prove poor colony health.',
   71:         'Honey ranking is cumulative recorded kilograms, not normalized yield per hive-day or per colony strength.',
   72:         'Attention flags are prompts for human review, not veterinary or apicultural diagnoses.',
   73:       ],
   74:     };
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   async productivity(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   78:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   79:     const harvests: any[] = await this.harvests.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ harvestDate: 1 }).limit(10000).lean();
   80:     const byMonth = new Map<string, number>();
   81:     const byHive = new Map<string, number>();
   82:     for (const row of harvests) {
   83:       const date = new Date(row.harvestDate);
   84:       if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) continue;
   85:       const month = `${date.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   86:       byMonth.set(month, (byMonth.get(month) || 0) + Number(row.quantityKg || 0));
   87:       byHive.set(row.hiveId, (byHive.get(row.hiveId) || 0) + Number(row.quantityKg || 0));
   88:     }
   89:     return {
   90:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   91:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   92:       monthlyRecordedHoneyKg: [...byMonth.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
   93:         .map(([month, quantityKg]) => ({ month, quantityKg: Math.round(quantityKg * 100) / 100 })),
   94:       hiveTotals: [...byHive.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
   95:         .map(([hiveId, quantityKg]) => ({ hiveId, quantityKg: Math.round(quantityKg * 100) / 100 })),
   96:       note: 'Monthly totals are grouped by UTC month. They reflect recorded harvests only.',
   97:     };
   98:   }
   99: }

   55:     }
   56:     if (dto.moisturePercent !== undefined && dto.moisturePercent !== null &&
   57:       (!Number.isFinite(Number(dto.moisturePercent)) || Number(dto.moisturePercent) < 0 || Number(dto.moisturePercent) > 100)) {
   58:       throw new BadRequestException('moisturePercent must be between 0 and 100');
   59:     }
   60:     await this.assertHive(context, dto.hiveId);
   61:     const payload = organizationDocument(context, {
   62:       ...dto,
   63:       quantityKg: Number(dto.quantityKg),
   64:       harvestedBy: dto.harvestedBy || actor,
   65:     } as Record<string, unknown>);
   66:     return this.harvests.create(payload);
   67:   }
   68: 
   69:   async summary(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   70:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   71:     const rows: any[] = await this.harvests.find(organizationFilter(context)).lean();
   72:     const totalKg = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + Number(row.quantityKg || 0), 0);
   73:     const byHive = new Map<string, number>();
   74:     for (const row of rows) byHive.set(row.hiveId, (byHive.get(row.hiveId) || 0) + Number(row.quantityKg || 0));
   75:     return {
   76:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   77:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   78:       harvestRecords: rows.length,
   79:       totalHoneyKg: Math.round(totalKg * 100) / 100,
   80:       hivesWithHarvests: byHive.size,
   81:       byHive: [...byHive.entries()].map(([hiveId, quantityKg]) => ({
   82:         hiveId, quantityKg: Math.round(quantityKg * 100) / 100,
   83:       })).sort((a, b) => a.hiveId.localeCompare(b.hiveId)),
   84:       note: 'Summary uses recorded harvest quantities only; it does not estimate unrecorded production or sales revenue.',
   85:     };
   86:   }
   87: }

   31:   constructor(
   32:     @InjectModel(BiologicalAsset.name) private assets: Model<BiologicalAsset>,

   36: 
   37:   async list(rawContext: OrganizationContext): Promise<BiologicalAsset[]> {
   38:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   39:     return this.assets.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ blockId: 1 }).lean();
   40:   }
   41: 
   42:   async listValuations(rawContext: OrganizationContext, blockId?: string): Promise<BiologicalValuation[]> {
   43:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   44:     const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   45:     if (blockId) filter.blockId = blockId;
   46:     return this.valuations.find(organizationFilter(context, filter)).sort({ valuationDate: -1 }).limit(200).lean();
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   async createAsset(
   50:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   51:     input: CreateAssetInput,
   52:     actor: string,
   53:   ): Promise<BiologicalAsset> {
   54:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   55:     const fairValue = input.estimatedVolume * input.fairValuePerUnitUgx;
   56: 
   57:     // 1. Create the asset record
   58:     const asset = await this.assets.create(
   59:       organizationDocument(context, {
   60:         blockId: input.blockId,
   61:         plantedDate: new Date(input.plantedDate),
   62:         areaAcres: input.areaAcres,
   63:         species: input.species,
   64:         costBasisUgx: input.costBasisUgx,
   65:         fairValueUgx: fairValue,
   66:         estimatedVolume: input.estimatedVolume,
   67:         volumeUnit: input.volumeUnit,
   68:         fairValuePerUnitUgx: input.fairValuePerUnitUgx,
   69:         createdBy: actor,
   70:       }),
   71:     );
   72: 
   73:     // 2. Post initial capitalisation + initial valuation
   74:     // First: capitalise cost basis
   75:     if (input.costBasisUgx > 0) {
   76:       await this.gl.postCapitalisation(context, asset.toObject(), actor);
   77:     }
   78: 
   79:     // Then: post initial fair value as a valuation (gain)
   80:     if (fairValue > 0) {
   81:       const valuation = await this.valuations.create(
   82:         organizationDocument(context, {
   83:           blockId: input.blockId,
   84:           valuationDate: new Date(input.plantedDate),
   85:           previousFairValueUgx: 0,
   86:           newFairValueUgx: fairValue,
   87:           gainLossUgx: fairValue,
   88:           estimatedVolume: input.estimatedVolume,
   89:           volumeUnit: input.volumeUnit,
   90:           fairValuePerUnitUgx: input.fairValuePerUnitUgx,
   91:           valuationMethod: 'market',
   92:           notes: 'Initial valuation on capitalisation',
   93:           createdBy: actor,
   94:         }),
   95:       );
   96:       await this.gl.postValuation(context, valuation.toObject());
   97: 
   98:       await this.assets.updateOne(
   99:         { _id: asset._id },
  100:         { $set: { lastValuationDate: new Date(input.plantedDate), lastValuationBy: actor } },
  101:       );
  102:     }
  103: 
  104:     return this.assets.findById(asset._id).lean() as any;
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   async revalue(
  108:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  109:     input: CreateValuationInput,
  110:     actor: string,
  111:   ): Promise<BiologicalValuation> {
  112:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  113:     const asset = await this.assets.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { blockId: input.blockId }));
  114:     if (!asset) throw new NotFoundException(`Biological asset for block ${input.blockId} not found`);
  115: 
  116:     const newFairValue = input.estimatedVolume * input.fairValuePerUnitUgx;
  117:     const previousFairValue = asset.fairValueUgx;
  118:     const gainLoss = newFairValue - previousFairValue;
  119: 
  120:     const valuation = await this.valuations.create(
  121:       organizationDocument(context, {
  122:         blockId: input.blockId,
  123:         valuationDate: new Date(input.valuationDate),
  124:         previousFairValueUgx: previousFairValue,
  125:         newFairValueUgx: newFairValue,
  126:         gainLossUgx: gainLoss,
  127:         estimatedVolume: input.estimatedVolume,
  128:         volumeUnit: asset.volumeUnit,
  129:         fairValuePerUnitUgx: input.fairValuePerUnitUgx,
  130:         valuationMethod: input.valuationMethod ?? 'market',
  131:         notes: input.notes,
  132:         createdBy: actor,
  133:       }),
  134:     );
  135: 
  136:     // Post to GL if there's a change
  137:     if (gainLoss !== 0) {
  138:       await this.gl.postValuation(context, valuation.toObject());
  139:     }
  140: 
  141:     // Update asset
  142:     await this.assets.updateOne(
  143:       { _id: asset._id },
  144:       {
  145:         $set: {
  146:           fairValueUgx: newFairValue,
  147:           estimatedVolume: input.estimatedVolume,
  148:           fairValuePerUnitUgx: input.fairValuePerUnitUgx,
  149:           lastValuationDate: new Date(input.valuationDate),
  150:           lastValuationBy: actor,
  151:         },
  152:       },
  153:     );
  154: 
  155:     return this.valuations.findById(valuation._id).lean() as any;
  156:   }
  157: 
  158:   private async postCapitalisation(
  159:     context: OrganizationContext,
  160:     asset: BiologicalAsset,
  161:     actor: string,
  162:   ): Promise<void> {
  163:     // Dr Biological Assets 1300 / Cr Owner's Capital 3000 (initial cost basis)
  164:     // In a production system this would credit the actual source: Cash, AP, etc.
  165:     // For a first-pass capitalisation, we credit Owner's Capital because the cost
  166:     // was already incurred before the asset was put on the books.
  167:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
  168:     await this.gl['posting'].post(ctx, {
  169:       date: new Date(asset.plantedDate),
  170:       description: `Capitalise biological asset: ${asset.blockId}`,
  171:       sourceType: 'BiologicalAssetCapitalisation',
  172:       sourceId: String(asset._id),
  173:       createdBy: actor,
  174:       lines: [
  175:         { accountCode: '1300', debit: asset.costBasisUgx, memo: `Cost basis for ${asset.blockId}` },
  176:         { accountCode: '3000', credit: asset.costBasisUgx, memo: `Capitalised into biological asset` },
  177:       ],
  178:     });
  179:   }
  180: }

    1: import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { PostingService } from '../accounting/posting.service';
    5: import { BiologicalAsset, BiologicalValuation } from './biological-assets.schema';
    6: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    7: 
    8: @Injectable()
    9: export class BiologicalAssetsGlService {
   10:   private readonly logger = new Logger(BiologicalAssetsGlService.name);
   11: 
   12:   constructor(
   13:     private readonly posting: PostingService,
   14:     @InjectModel(BiologicalAsset.name) private assets: Model<BiologicalAsset>,
   15:     @InjectModel(BiologicalValuation.name) private valuations: Model<BiologicalValuation>,
   16:   ) {}
   17: 
   18:   /**
   19:    * Post a revaluation. If gain (positive), Dr Biological Assets / Cr Revaluation Gain (revenue).
   20:    * If loss (negative), Dr Revaluation Loss (expense) / Cr Biological Assets.
   21:    */
   22: 
   23:   // In BiologicalAssetsGlService:
   24: async postCapitalisation(
   25:   context: OrganizationContext,
   26:   asset: BiologicalAsset,
   27:   actor: string,
   28: ): Promise<void> {
   29:   const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   30:   await this.posting.post(ctx, {
   31:     date: new Date(asset.plantedDate),
   32:     description: `Capitalise biological asset: ${asset.blockId}`,
   33:     sourceType: 'BiologicalAssetCapitalisation',
   34:     sourceId: String(asset._id),
   35:     createdBy: actor,
   36:     lines: [
   37:       { accountCode: '1300', debit: asset.costBasisUgx, memo: `Cost basis for ${asset.blockId}` },
   38:       { accountCode: '3000', credit: asset.costBasisUgx, memo: `Capitalised into biological asset` },
   39:     ],
   40:   });
   41: }
   42: 
   43:   async postValuation(
   44:     context: OrganizationContext,
   45:     valuation: BiologicalValuation,
   46:   ): Promise<void> {
   47:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   48:     if (valuation.gainLossUgx === 0) return;   // no-op
   49: 
   50:     const lines = valuation.gainLossUgx > 0
   51:       ? [
   52:           { accountCode: '1300', debit: valuation.gainLossUgx, memo: `Revaluation gain: ${valuation.blockId}` },
   53:           { accountCode: '4030', credit: valuation.gainLossUgx, memo: `Growth revaluation: ${valuation.blockId}` },
   54:         ]
   55:       : [
   56:           { accountCode: '6320', debit: Math.abs(valuation.gainLossUgx), memo: `Revaluation loss: ${valuation.blockId}` },
   57:           { accountCode: '1300', credit: Math.abs(valuation.gainLossUgx), memo: `Impairment: ${valuation.blockId}` },
   58:         ];
   59: 
   60:     const entry = await this.posting.post(ctx, {
   61:       date: new Date(valuation.valuationDate),
   62:       description: `Biological asset revaluation: ${valuation.blockId}`,
   63:       sourceType: 'BiologicalValuation',
   64:       sourceId: String(valuation._id),
   65:       createdBy: valuation.createdBy,
   66:       lines,
   67:     });
   68: 
   69:     await this.valuations.updateOne(
   70:       { _id: valuation._id },
   71:       { $set: { journalEntryId: entry._id } },
   72:     );
   73:   }
   74: }

   27:   async summary(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   28:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   29:     const filter = organizationFilter(context);
   30: 
   31:     console.log('org filter:', JSON.stringify(filter));
   32: 
   33:     const [transactions, orders, items, tasks, inspections, evidenceCount] = await Promise.all([
   34:       // Scoped â€” these schemas already have organizationId
   35:       this.finance.find(filter).lean(),
   36:       this.sales.find(filter).lean(),
   37:       this.inventory.find(filter).lean(),
   38: 
   39:       // âš ï¸ TEMPORARY â€” these schemas do not yet have organizationId.
   40:       // After adding the field + backfilling + scoping their services,

   50:     const income = transactions.filter(x => x.direction === 'Income').reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.amountUgx || 0), 0);

   54:     const salesPaid = orders.filter(x => x.status !== 'Cancelled').reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.amountPaidUgx || 0), 0);

   59:     const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
   60:     const sevenDays = new Date(today); sevenDays.setDate(sevenDays.getDate() + 7);
   61:     const openInspections = inspections.filter((x: any) => x.status !== 'Completed');
   62:     const overdueInspections = openInspections.filter((x: any) => new Date(x.dueDate) < today);
   63:     const dueSoonInspections = openInspections.filter((x: any) => new Date(x.dueDate) >= today && new Date(x.dueDate) <= sevenDays);
   64:     const highRiskInspections = openInspections.filter((x: any) => ['High', 'Critical'].includes(x.priority));
   65:     const monthly: Record<string, { incomeUgx: number; expensesUgx: number; netUgx: number }> = {};
   66:     for (const tx of transactions as any[]) {
   67:       const date = new Date(tx.date); if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) continue;
   68:       const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   69:       if (!monthly[key]) monthly[key] = { incomeUgx: 0, expensesUgx: 0, netUgx: 0 };
   70:       if (tx.direction === 'Income') monthly[key].incomeUgx += Number(tx.amountUgx || 0);
   71:       if (tx.direction === 'Expense') monthly[key].expensesUgx += Number(tx.amountUgx || 0);
   72:     }
   73:     Object.values(monthly).forEach(v => v.netUgx = v.incomeUgx - v.expensesUgx);
   74:     const byLine: Record<string, { income: number; expenses: number; net: number }> = {};
   75:     for (const tx of transactions) {
   76:       const line = tx.businessLine || 'Unclassified';
   77:       if (!byLine[line]) byLine[line] = { income: 0, expenses: 0, net: 0 };
   78:       if (tx.direction === 'Income') byLine[line].income += Number(tx.amountUgx || 0);
   79:       if (tx.direction === 'Expense') byLine[line].expenses += Number(tx.amountUgx || 0);
   80:     }
   81:     Object.values(byLine).forEach(v => v.net = v.income - v.expenses);
   82:     return {
   83:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   84:       finance: { recordedIncomeUgx: income, recordedExpensesUgx: expenses, operatingNetUgx: income - expenses, transfersUgx: transfers },
   85:       sales: { orderValueUgx: salesValue, amountPaidUgx: salesPaid, outstandingUgx: outstanding, orderCount: orders.length },
   86:       operations: { inventoryItems: items.length, lowStockItems: lowStock, openTasks, overdueTasks },
   87:       inspections: { total: inspections.length, open: openInspections.length, overdue: overdueInspections.length, dueWithin7Days: dueSoonInspections.length, highOrCriticalOpen: highRiskInspections.length, evidenceCount },
   88:       monthlyFinancialTrend: Object.entries(monthly).sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).slice(-12).map(([month, values]) => ({ month, ...values })),
   89:       attentionItems: [
   90:         ...overdueInspections.slice(0, 10).map((x: any) => ({ type: 'Overdue inspection', priority: 'High', title: x.title, siteOrBlock: x.siteOrBlock, dueDate: x.dueDate, status: x.status })),
   91:         ...highRiskInspections.slice(0, 10).map((x: any) => ({ type: 'High-risk inspection', priority: x.priority, title: x.title, siteOrBlock: x.siteOrBlock, dueDate: x.dueDate, status: x.status })),
   92:         ...tasks.filter((x: any) => !['Completed', 'Cancelled'].includes(x.status) && x.dueDate && new Date(x.dueDate) < today).slice(0, 10).map((x: any) => ({ type: 'Overdue task', priority: 'Medium', title: x.title || x.taskName || 'Field task', siteOrBlock: x.siteOrBlock || '', dueDate: x.dueDate, status: x.status })),
   93:       ].slice(0, 20),
   94:       performanceByBusinessLine: byLine,
   95:       warnings: [
   96:         'Cash balance is not calculated from transactions alone until opening balances and transfers are reconciled.',
   97:         'Sales order value and finance income are separate views; do not add them together.',
   98:         'This dashboard summarizes recorded data and may be incomplete if transactions are missing.',
   99:       ],
  100:     };
  101:   }
  102: }

    1: import { ForbiddenException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

   12: type CurrentUser = { email: string; role: string };
   13: 
   14: @Injectable()
   15: export class EvidenceService {
   16:   constructor(@InjectModel(Evidence.name) private model: Model<Evidence>) {}
   17: 
   18:   async list(
   19:     inspectionId: string | undefined,
   20:     user: CurrentUser,
   21:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   22:   ) {
   23:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   24: 
   25:     const roleFilter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   26:     if (inspectionId) roleFilter.inspectionId = inspectionId;
   27:     if (user.role !== 'owner') roleFilter.uploadedBy = user.email.toLowerCase();
   28: 
   29:     const filter = organizationFilter(context, roleFilter);
   30:     return this.model
   31:       .find(filter)
   32:       .select('-dataBase64')
   33:       .sort({ capturedAt: -1 })
   34:       .limit(100)
   35:       .lean();
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   async create(
   39:     dto: CreateEvidenceDto,
   40:     user: CurrentUser,
   41:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   42:   ) {
   43:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   44: 
   45:     const raw = String(dto.dataBase64 || '').replace(
   46:       /^data:image\/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,/,
   47:       '',
   48:     );
   49:     if (!raw || raw.length > 5_000_000) {
   50:       throw new ForbiddenException(
   51:         'Image payload too large (max about 3.5 MB encoded image)',
   52:       );
   53:     }
   54:     const buf = Buffer.from(raw, 'base64');
   55:     if (!buf.length || buf.length > 3_500_000) {
   56:       throw new ForbiddenException('Image must be smaller than 3.5 MB');
   57:     }
   58: 
   59:     // Note: we deliberately do NOT verify that `inspectionId` belongs to this
   60:     // org. That's a separate validation â€” if you want it, inject the Inspection
   61:     // model and check `findOne({ _id: dto.inspectionId, organizationId })`.
   62:     // The evidence is still org-scoped so it can't leak cross-tenant.
   63:     return this.model.create({
   64:       ...dto,
   65:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   66:       dataBase64: raw,
   67:       uploadedBy: user.email.toLowerCase(),
   68:       capturedAt: dto.capturedAt ? new Date(dto.capturedAt) : new Date(),
   69:     });
   70:   }
   71: 
   72:   async image(
   73:     id: string,
   74:     user: CurrentUser,
   75:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   76:   ) {

   80:     if (user.role !== 'owner') roleFilter.uploadedBy = user.email.toLowerCase();
   81:

   83:     const doc = await this.model.findOne(filter).lean();
   84:     if (!doc) throw new NotFoundException('Evidence not found');

   27:   private async audit(context: any, captureId: string, evidenceId: string, actorId: string, eventType: 'uploaded' | 'downloaded' | 'deleted', outcome: 'success' | 'denied' | 'failed', details: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {

   32:   private async capture(raw: OrganizationContext, id: string) {

   34:     if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) throw new NotFoundException('Capture not found');
