   41:     if (!file || !Buffer.isBuffer(file.buffer) || !file.buffer.length) throw new BadRequestException('Select a file to upload');
   42:     if (file.buffer.length > MAX_BYTES) throw new BadRequestException('File exceeds the 15 MB limit');
   43:     const detected = Object.values(TYPES).find(t => t.signature(file.buffer));
   44:     if (!detected) throw new BadRequestException('Unsupported or invalid file signature; JPEG, PNG, WebP or PDF only');
   45:     if (file.mimetype && file.mimetype !== detected.mime) throw new BadRequestException('File content does not match its declared MIME type');
   46:     const { context, capture } = await this.capture(raw, id);
   47:     if ((capture.evidenceManifest || []).length >= 10) throw new BadRequestException('A maximum of 10 evidence files is allowed per capture');
   48:     const orgCaptures = await this.captures.find(organizationFilter(context, {})).select({ evidenceManifest: 1 }).lean();
   49:     const usedBytes = orgCaptures.reduce((sum: number, row: any) =>
   50:       sum + (row.evidenceManifest || []).filter((e: any) => !e.deletedAt).reduce((subtotal: number, e: any) => subtotal + (Number(e.sizeBytes) || 0), 0), 0);
   51:     if (!Number.isFinite(ORG_QUOTA_BYTES) || ORG_QUOTA_BYTES <= 0) throw new BadRequestException('Evidence storage quota configuration is invalid');
   52:     if (usedBytes + file.buffer.length > ORG_QUOTA_BYTES) throw new ConflictException('Organization evidence storage quota would be exceeded');
   53:     const evidenceId = randomUUID();
   54:     const safeOrg = context.organizationId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '');
   55:     const dir = resolve(ROOT, safeOrg);
   56:     if (!dir.startsWith(ROOT + sep)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid storage scope');
   57:     await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
   58:     const fileName = `${evidenceId}${detected.ext}`;
   59:     const fullPath = resolve(dir, fileName);
   60:     if (!fullPath.startsWith(dir + sep)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid storage path');
   61:     // File bytes are written under a server-generated name, never the client filename.
   62:     await writeFile(fullPath, file.buffer, { flag: 'wx', mode: 0o600 });
   63:     const digest = createHash('sha256').update(file.buffer).digest('hex');
   64:     const item = {
   65:       evidenceId, filename: String(file.originalname || 'evidence').replace(/[\\/\x00-\x1f]/g, '_').slice(0,200),
   66:       mimeType: detected.mime, sizeBytes: file.buffer.length, sha256: digest,
   67:       storageKey: `${safeOrg}/${fileName}`, uploadedAt: new Date().toISOString(), uploadedBy: actorId,
   68:     };
   69:     try {
   70:       const updated = await this.captures.findOneAndUpdate(
   71:         organizationFilter(context, {
   72:           _id: capture._id,
   73:           $expr: { $lt: [{ $size: { $ifNull: ['$evidenceManifest', []] } }, 10] },
   74:         }),
   75:         { $push: { evidenceManifest: item } },
   76:         { new: true },
   77:       );
   78:       if (!updated) throw new ConflictException('Capture evidence limit reached or capture changed; retry after refreshing');
   79:     } catch (error) {
   80:       // Avoid leaving an unreferenced file when metadata persistence fails.
   81:       try { const { unlink } = await import('fs/promises'); await unlink(fullPath); } catch {}
   82:       throw error;
   83:     }
   84:     await this.audit(context, String(capture._id), evidenceId, actorId, 'uploaded', 'success', { sizeBytes: item.sizeBytes, mimeType: item.mimeType });
   85:     return { evidenceId, filename: item.filename, mimeType: item.mimeType, sizeBytes: item.sizeBytes, sha256: item.sha256 };
   86:   }
   87: 
   88:   async getDownload(id: string, evidenceId: string, raw: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {
   89:     const { context, capture } = await this.capture(raw, id);
   90:     const item: any = (capture.evidenceManifest || []).find((x: any) => x.evidenceId === evidenceId);
   91:     if (!item || item.deletedAt || typeof item.storageKey !== 'string') throw new NotFoundException('Evidence file not found');
   92:     const safeOrg = context.organizationId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '');
   93:     const expectedPrefix = `${safeOrg}/`;
   94:     if (!item.storageKey.startsWith(expectedPrefix) || item.storageKey.includes('..')) throw new NotFoundException('Evidence file not found');
   95:     const fullPath = resolve(ROOT, item.storageKey);
   96:     const orgRoot = resolve(ROOT, safeOrg);
   97:     if (!fullPath.startsWith(orgRoot + sep)) throw new NotFoundException('Evidence file not found');
   98:     try {
   99:       const [rootReal, fileReal, info] = await Promise.all([realpath(orgRoot), realpath(fullPath), stat(fullPath)]);
  100:       if (!fileReal.startsWith(rootReal + sep) || !info.isFile() || info.size > MAX_BYTES) throw new NotFoundException('Evidence file not found');
  101:       const bytes = await readFile(fileReal);
  102:       if (createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex') !== item.sha256) throw new NotFoundException('Evidence integrity check failed');
  103:       await this.audit(context, String(capture._id), evidenceId, actorId, 'downloaded', 'success', { sizeBytes: info.size });
  104:       return { bytes, mimeType: item.mimeType, filename: item.filename };
  105:     } catch (e: any) {
  106:       if (e?.status) throw e;
  107:       throw new NotFoundException('Evidence file is unavailable');
  108:     }
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   async deleteEvidence(id: string, evidenceId: string, raw: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {
  112:     const { context, capture } = await this.capture(raw, id);
  113:     const item: any = (capture.evidenceManifest || []).find((x: any) => x.evidenceId === evidenceId && !x.deletedAt);
  114:     if (!item) throw new NotFoundException('Evidence file not found');
  115:     // Soft-delete keeps the object for controlled recovery/retention handling.
  116:     const now = new Date().toISOString();
  117:     const result = await this.captures.updateOne(
  118:       organizationFilter(context, { _id: capture._id, 'evidenceManifest.evidenceId': evidenceId, 'evidenceManifest.deletedAt': { $exists: false } }),
  119:       { $set: {
  120:         'evidenceManifest.$.deletedAt': now,
  121:         'evidenceManifest.$.deletedBy': actorId,
  122:       } },
  123:     );
  124:     if (!result.modifiedCount) throw new ConflictException('Evidence was already changed or deleted; refresh and retry');
  125:     await this.audit(context, String(capture._id), evidenceId, actorId, 'deleted', 'success', {
  126:       sizeBytes: Number(item.sizeBytes) || 0, filename: String(item.filename || ''), softDelete: true,
  127:     });
  128:     return { deleted: true, recoverable: true, evidenceId, deletedAt: now };
  129:   }
  130: 
  131:   async governanceSummary(raw: OrganizationContext) {
  132:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(raw);
  133:     const rows = await this.captures.find(organizationFilter(context, {})).select({ _id: 1, evidenceManifest: 1 }).lean();
  134:     const allEvidence = rows.flatMap((capture: any) => (capture.evidenceManifest || []).map((item: any) => ({
  135:       captureId: String(capture._id), evidenceId: String(item.evidenceId || ''),
  136:       sizeBytes: Number(item.sizeBytes) || 0, uploadedAt: item.uploadedAt || null,
  137:       mimeType: item.mimeType || 'unknown', hasStorageKey: typeof item.storageKey === 'string', deletedAt: item.deletedAt || null,
  138:     })));
  139:     const evidence = allEvidence.filter((item: any) => !item.deletedAt);
  140:     const events = await this.events.find({ organizationId: context.organizationId }).sort({ createdAt: -1 }).limit(100).lean();
  141:     return {
  142:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
  143:       evidenceCount: evidence.length,
  144:       softDeletedCount: allEvidence.filter((item: any) => !!item.deletedAt).length,
  145:       quotaBytes: ORG_QUOTA_BYTES,
  146:       quotaRemainingBytes: Math.max(0, ORG_QUOTA_BYTES - evidence.reduce((sum: number, item: any) => sum + item.sizeBytes, 0)),
  147:       totalBytes: evidence.reduce((sum: number, item: any) => sum + item.sizeBytes, 0),
  148:       metadataOnlyCount: evidence.filter((item: any) => !item.hasStorageKey).length,
  149:       recentEvents: events.map((event: any) => ({
  150:         eventType: event.eventType, outcome: event.outcome, captureId: event.captureId,
  151:         evidenceId: event.evidenceId, actorId: event.actorId, createdAt: event.createdAt,
  152:         details: event.details,
  153:       })),
  154:     };
  155:   }

   21:   private async assertTask(c: OrganizationContext, taskId: string) {
   22:     const task = await this.tasks.findOne(organizationFilter(c, { taskId })).lean();
   23:     if (!task) throw new NotFoundException('Task not found in selected organization');

   27:   async submit(dto: any, raw: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {

   30:       throw new BadRequestException('clientSubmissionId is required (max 120 characters) for retry-safe submission');
   31:     }

   33:     if (!CAPTURE_TYPES.includes(dto.captureType)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid captureType');
   34:     if (dto.notes !== undefined && (typeof dto.notes !== 'string' || dto.notes.length > 5000)) throw new BadRequestException('notes must be text up to 5000 characters');
   35:     const prior = await this.captures.findOne(organizationFilter(c, { clientSubmissionId: dto.clientSubmissionId })).lean();
   36:     if (prior) return { ...prior, duplicateSubmission: true };
   37:     const task: any = await this.assertTask(c, dto.taskId);
   38:     const assignedIdentityMatches = task.workerId === actorId;
   39:     if (!this.isManager(c) && !assignedIdentityMatches) throw new ForbiddenException('Only the assigned worker or a manager may submit evidence for this task');
   40: 
   41:     const manifest = Array.isArray(dto.evidenceManifest) ? dto.evidenceManifest : [];
   42:     if (manifest.length > 10) throw new BadRequestException('A maximum of 10 evidence manifest entries is allowed');
   43:     const safeManifest = manifest.map((item: any) => {
   44:       if (!item || typeof item.filename !== 'string' || item.filename.length > 200 ||
   45:           !MIME_TYPES.includes(item.mimeType) ||
   46:           !Number.isInteger(item.sizeBytes) || item.sizeBytes < 1 || item.sizeBytes > 15 * 1024 * 1024 ||
   47:           typeof item.sha256 !== 'string' || !/^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$/.test(item.sha256)) {

   49:       }
   50:       return { filename: item.filename, mimeType: item.mimeType, sizeBytes: item.sizeBytes, sha256: item.sha256 };
   51:     });
   52:

   54:     const locationConsent = dto.locationConsent === true;

   58:         throw new BadRequestException('Location coordinates are invalid');

   62:     const captureId = `FC-${new Types.ObjectId().toHexString()}`;
   63:     const created = await this.captures.create({
   64:       organizationId: c.organizationId, captureId, clientSubmissionId: dto.clientSubmissionId,

   27:     if (!worker) throw new NotFoundException('Active worker not found in selected organization');
   28:     return worker;
   29:   }

   49:   async listTasks(raw: OrganizationContext, filters: any = {}) {

   52:     if (filters.status) {
   53:       if (!STATUSES.includes(filters.status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid status filter');

   55:     }

   59:       query.status = { $nin: ['Approved', 'Cancelled'] };
   60:     }
   61:     const limit = Math.min(500, Math.max(1, Number(filters.limit) || 100));
   62:     return this.tasks.find(query).sort({ dueDate: 1, createdAt: -1 }).limit(limit).lean();
   63:   }

   65:   async createTask(dto: any, raw: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {
   66:     const c = requireOrganizationContext(raw); this.manager(c);
   67:     if (!dto?.title?.trim() || !dto?.workerId) throw new BadRequestException('title and workerId are required');
   68:     if (dto.priority && !PRIORITIES.includes(dto.priority)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid priority');
   69:     if (dto.dueDate && Number.isNaN(Date.parse(dto.dueDate))) throw new BadRequestException('dueDate must be a valid date');
   70:     await this.worker(c, dto.workerId);

   74:     }
   75:     const taskId = `FT-${new Types.ObjectId().toHexString()}`;

   77:       organizationId: c.organizationId, taskId, title: dto.title.trim(), description: dto.description,
   78:       priority: dto.priority || 'Medium', status: 'Assigned', workerId: dto.workerId,
   79:       apiaryActionId: dto.apiaryActionId, dueDate: dto.dueDate ? new Date(dto.dueDate) : undefined,
   80:       createdBy: actorId, updatedBy: actorId,
   81:     });
   82:     await this.log(c, taskId, actorId, 'assigned', { workerId: dto.workerId, priority: task.priority });
   83:     return task;
   84:   }
   85: 
   86:   async transition(id: string, dto: any, raw: OrganizationContext, actorId: string) {
   87:     const c = requireOrganizationContext(raw);
   88:     const task = await this.tasks.findOne(organizationFilter(c, { _id: id }));
   89:     if (!task) throw new NotFoundException('Task not found in selected organization');
   90:     const manager = hasOrganizationRole(c, MANAGER_ROLES);
   91:     const ownWorker = task.workerId === actorId;
   92:     const status = dto?.status;
   93:     if (!status || !STATUSES.includes(status)) throw new BadRequestException('A valid status is required');
   94:     const managerOnly = ['Assigned', 'Approved', 'Rejected', 'Cancelled'];
   95:     if (managerOnly.includes(status) && !manager) throw new ForbiddenException('Manager or supervisor role required for this transition');
   96:     if (!manager && !ownWorker) throw new ForbiddenException('Only the assigned worker or a manager may update this task');
   97:     const allowed: Record<string, string[]> = {
   98:       Assigned: ['Accepted', 'Cancelled'],
   99:       Accepted: ['In progress', 'Blocked', 'Cancelled'],
  100:       'In progress': ['Blocked', 'Submitted', 'Cancelled'],
  101:       Blocked: ['In progress', 'Cancelled'],
  102:       Submitted: ['Approved', 'Rejected'],
  103:       Rejected: ['In progress', 'Cancelled'],
  104:       Approved: [],
  105:       Cancelled: [],
  106:     };
  107:     if (!manager && !(allowed[task.status] || []).includes(status)) {
  108:       throw new BadRequestException(`Transition ${task.status} -> ${status} is not allowed`);
  109:     }
  110:     if (manager && !STATUSES.includes(status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid status');
  111:     if (status === 'Accepted' && !manager && !ownWorker) throw new ForbiddenException('Only assigned worker may accept');
  112:     const before = task.status;
  113:     task.status = status;
  114:     task.updatedBy = actorId;
  115:     if (status === 'In progress' && !task.startedAt) task.startedAt = new Date();
  116:     if (status === 'Submitted') {
  117:       task.submittedAt = new Date();
  118:       if (dto.completionNotes !== undefined) task.completionNotes = String(dto.completionNotes).slice(0, 2000);
  119:       if (dto.evidenceReferences !== undefined) {
  120:         if (!Array.isArray(dto.evidenceReferences) || dto.evidenceReferences.some((x: any) => typeof x !== 'string' || x.length > 500)) {
  121:           throw new BadRequestException('evidenceReferences must be an array of strings, each at most 500 characters');
  122:         }
  123:         task.evidenceReferences = dto.evidenceReferences.slice(0, 20);
  124:       }
  125:     }
  126:     if (status === 'Approved' || status === 'Rejected') {
  127:       task.reviewedAt = new Date();
  128:       if (dto.reviewNotes !== undefined) task.reviewNotes = String(dto.reviewNotes).slice(0, 1000);
  129:     }
  130:     await task.save();
  131:     await this.log(c, task.taskId, actorId, 'status_changed', { before, after: status }, dto.note);
  132:     return task;
  133:   }
  134: 
  135:   async summary(raw: OrganizationContext) {
  136:     const c = requireOrganizationContext(raw);
  137:     const rows: any[] = await this.tasks.find(organizationFilter(c)).select('status priority dueDate').limit(10000).lean();
  138:     const result: any = { total: rows.length, overdue: 0, byStatus: {} };
  139:     for (const s of STATUSES) result.byStatus[s] = 0;
  140:     for (const r of rows) {
  141:       result.byStatus[r.status] = (result.byStatus[r.status] || 0) + 1;
  142:       if (!['Approved', 'Cancelled'].includes(r.status) && r.dueDate && new Date(r.dueDate).getTime() < Date.now()) result.overdue++;
  143:     }
  144:     return result;
  145:   }
  146: 
  147:   async history(id: string, raw: OrganizationContext) {
  148:     const c = requireOrganizationContext(raw);
  149:     const task = await this.tasks.findOne(organizationFilter(c, { _id: id })).select('taskId').lean();
  150:     if (!task) throw new NotFoundException('Task not found in selected organization');
  151:     return this.events.find({ organizationId: c.organizationId, taskId: task.taskId }).sort({ createdAt: -1 }).limit(500).lean();
  152:   }

    1: import { BadRequestException, Injectable, Logger, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { FinanceTransaction } from './finance.schema';
    5: import { CreateFinanceDto } from './dto';
    6: import { AuditService } from '../audit/audit.service';
    7: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    8: import { organizationDocument, organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
    9: import { FinanceGlAdapter } from '../accounting/finance-gl-adapter.service';
   10: 
   11: /** Fields that, once a JournalEntry exists for the transaction, cannot be changed. */
   12: const GL_LOCKED_FIELDS = [
   13:   'amountUgx',
   14:   'category',
   15:   'direction',
   16:   'businessLine',
   17:   'paymentMethod',
   18:   'date',
   19: ] as const;
   20: 
   21: @Injectable()
   22: export class FinanceService {
   23:   private readonly logger = new Logger(FinanceService.name);
   24: 
   25:   constructor(
   26:     @InjectModel(FinanceTransaction.name) private model: Model<FinanceTransaction>,
   27:     private readonly audit: AuditService,
   28:     private readonly gl: FinanceGlAdapter,
   29:   ) {}
   30: 
   31:   findAll(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   32:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   33:     return this.model.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ date: -1 }).limit(500).lean();
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   async create(dto: CreateFinanceDto, rawContext: OrganizationContext, actorEmail: string) {
   37:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   38:     const payload = organizationDocument(context, dto as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
   39:     let created: any;
   40: 
   41:     // Step 1: create the Finance doc â€” duplicates throw here
   42:     try {
   43:       created = await this.model.create(payload);
   44:     } catch (e: any) {
   45:       if (e?.code === 11000) {
   46:         throw new BadRequestException(
   47:           `Transaction "${dto.transactionId}" already exists in this organization`,
   48:         );
   49:       }
   50:       throw e;
   51:     }
   52: 
   53:     // Step 2: post to GL â€” only reached if create succeeded, so `created` exists
   54:     try {
   55:       await this.gl.postTransaction(context, created.toObject());
   56:     } catch (e: any) {
   57:       // Period locked â†’ reject the whole thing. This is a business rule, not a glitch.
   58:       if (e?.status === 400 && /locked period/i.test(e.message)) {
   59:         await this.model.deleteOne({ _id: created._id });
   60:         throw e;
   61:       }
   62: 
   63:       // Any other GL error â†’ log and proceed. The backfill script will sweep it up.
   64:       // Do NOT reject the Finance doc â€” it's already valid and users expect it saved.
   65:       this.logger.error(
   66:         `GL post failed for ${created.transactionId} (${created._id}): ${e.message}`,
   67:         e.stack,
   68:       );
   69:     }
   70: 
   71:     // Step 3: audit
   72:     await this.audit.record(
   73:       actorEmail,
   74:       'FINANCE_TRANSACTION_CREATED',
   75:       'FinanceTransaction',
   76:       String(created._id),
   77:       `Created finance transaction ${created.transactionId}`,
   78:       { after: this.auditSnapshot(created.toObject()) },
   79:     );
   80: 
   81:     return created;
   82:   }
   83: 
   84:   async update(id: string, dto: Partial<CreateFinanceDto>, rawContext: OrganizationContext, actorEmail: string) {
   85:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   86:     if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid transaction id');
   87: 
   88:     const before = await this.model.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: id })).lean();
   89:     if (!before) throw new NotFoundException('Transaction not found');
   90: 
   91:     // A transaction's human-readable identifier is immutable after creation.
   92:     const safeUpdate: Record<string, unknown> = { ...dto };
   93:     delete safeUpdate.transactionId;
   94:     delete (safeUpdate as any).organizationId;
   95:     delete (safeUpdate as any).createdBy;
   96: 
   97:     // GL lock: once a JournalEntry exists for this transaction, GL-affecting
   98:     // fields cannot be edited. Reverse-and-repost instead.
   99:     const glLockedAttempted = Object.keys(safeUpdate).filter(k =>
  100:       (GL_LOCKED_FIELDS as readonly string[]).includes(k),
  101:     );
  102:     if (glLockedAttempted.length > 0) {
  103:       const posted = await this.gl.hasPostedEntry(context, String(before._id));
  104:       if (posted) {
  105:         throw new BadRequestException(
  106:           `Cannot edit ${glLockedAttempted.join(', ')} on a posted transaction. ` +
  107:             `Void and recreate, or post a reversing entry. ` +
  108:             `Only 'reconciled', 'notes', 'counterparty', 'receiptReference' may be edited after posting.`,
  109:         );
  110:       }
  111:     }
  112: 
  113:     const after = await this.model.findOneAndUpdate(
  114:       organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }),
  115:       safeUpdate,
  116:       { new: true, runValidators: true },
  117:     ).lean();
  118:     if (!after) throw new NotFoundException('Transaction not found');
  119: 
  120:     // If GL-relevant fields changed AND no journal existed (edge case: GL post failed earlier),
  121:     // try to post now that the data is corrected.
  122:     if (glLockedAttempted.length > 0) {
  123:       try {
  124:         await this.gl.postTransaction(context, after);
  125:       } catch (e: any) {
  126:         this.logger.error(
  127:           `Late GL post failed for ${after.transactionId} (${after._id}): ${e.message}`,
  128:           e.stack,
  129:         );
  130:       }
  131:     }
  132: 
  133:     await this.audit.record(
  134:       actorEmail,
  135:       'FINANCE_TRANSACTION_UPDATED',
  136:       'FinanceTransaction',
  137:       String(after._id),
  138:       `Updated finance transaction ${after.transactionId}`,
  139:       {
  140:         before: this.auditSnapshot(before),
  141:         after: this.auditSnapshot(after),
  142:         requestedFields: Object.keys(safeUpdate),
  143:         glLockedBlocked: glLockedAttempted,
  144:       },
  145:     );
  146:     return after;
  147:   }
  148: 
  149:   private auditSnapshot(value: any) {
  150:     return {
  151:       transactionId: value.transactionId,
  152:       date: value.date,
  153:       direction: value.direction,
  154:       businessLine: value.businessLine,
  155:       category: value.category,
  156:       description: value.description,
  157:       amountUgx: value.amountUgx,
  158:       paymentMethod: value.paymentMethod,
  159:       counterparty: value.counterparty,
  160:       receiptReference: value.receiptReference,
  161:       reconciled: value.reconciled,
  162:       notes: value.notes,
  163:     };
  164:   }
  165: }

    1: import { BadRequestException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { GrowerContract, HarvestLot, LotSale } from './harvest-intake.schema';
    5: import { HarvestGlService } from './harvest-intake-gl.service';
    6: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    7: import { organizationDocument, organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
    8: 
    9: export type CreateHarvestLotInput = {
   10:   lotCode: string;
   11:   sourceType: 'own_farm' | 'grower';
   12:   blockId: string;
   13:   growerContractId?: string;
   14:   harvestDate: string;
   15:   commodityType: 'timber' | 'honey' | 'other';
   16:   unit: string;
   17:   quantity: number;
   18:   unitCostUgx: number;
   19:   grade?: string;
   20:   qualityNotes?: string;
   21: };
   22: 
   23: export type CreateSaleInput = {
   24:   lotId: string;
   25:   saleCode: string;
   26:   saleDate: string;
   27:   quantitySold: number;
   28:   unitPriceUgx: number;
   29:   buyerName?: string;
   30:   paymentStatus: 'paid' | 'pending' | 'partial';
   31: };
   32: 
   33: @Injectable()
   34: export class HarvestService {
   35:   constructor(
   36:     @InjectModel(GrowerContract.name) private contracts: Model<GrowerContract>,
   37:     @InjectModel(HarvestLot.name) private lots: Model<HarvestLot>,
   38:     @InjectModel(LotSale.name) private sales: Model<LotSale>,
   39:     private readonly gl: HarvestGlService,
   40:   ) {}
   41: 
   42:   async createLot(
   43:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   44:     input: CreateHarvestLotInput,
   45:     actor: string,
   46:   ): Promise<HarvestLot> {
   47:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   48:     if (!input.quantity || input.quantity <= 0) throw new BadRequestException('quantity must be > 0');
   49:     if (!input.unitCostUgx || input.unitCostUgx < 0) throw new BadRequestException('unitCostUgx must be >= 0');
   50: 
   51:     // Validate grower contract if source is grower
   52:     let contract: GrowerContract | null = null;
   53:     if (input.sourceType === 'grower') {
   54:       if (!input.growerContractId) throw new BadRequestException('growerContractId required for grower source');
   55:       contract = await this.contracts.findOne(
   56:         organizationFilter(context, { _id: input.growerContractId }),
   57:       );
   58:       if (!contract) throw new NotFoundException('Grower contract not found');
   59:       if (contract.status !== 'active') throw new BadRequestException(`Contract ${contract.contractCode} is ${contract.status}, not active`);
   60:     }
   61: 
   62:     const lot = await this.lots.create(
   63:       organizationDocument(context, {
   64:         lotCode: input.lotCode,
   65:         sourceType: input.sourceType,
   66:         blockId: input.blockId,
   67:         growerContractId: input.growerContractId ? new Types.ObjectId(input.growerContractId) : undefined,
   68:         harvestDate: new Date(input.harvestDate),
   69:         commodityType: input.commodityType,
   70:         unit: input.unit,
   71:         quantity: input.quantity,
   72:         originalQuantity: input.quantity,
   73:         unitCostUgx: input.unitCostUgx,
   74:         grade: input.grade,
   75:         qualityNotes: input.qualityNotes,
   76:         status: 'in_stock',
   77:         createdBy: actor,
   78:       }),
   79:     );
   80: 
   81:     // Post to GL
   82:     if (input.sourceType === 'own_farm') {
   83:       await this.gl.postOwnFarmHarvest(context, lot.toObject());
   84:     } else {
   85:       await this.gl.postGrowerHarvest(context, lot.toObject());
   86:     }
   87: 
   88:     return lot.toObject();
   89:   }
   90: 
   91:   async createSale(
   92:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
   93:     input: CreateSaleInput,
   94:     actor: string,
   95:   ): Promise<LotSale> {
   96:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   97: 
   98:     const lot = await this.lots.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: input.lotId }));
   99:     if (!lot) throw new NotFoundException('Lot not found');
  100:     if (lot.quantity < input.quantitySold) {
  101:       throw new BadRequestException(`Only ${lot.quantity} ${lot.unit} available, cannot sell ${input.quantitySold}`);
  102:     }
  103:     if (!input.quantitySold || input.quantitySold <= 0) throw new BadRequestException('quantitySold must be > 0');
  104:     if (!input.unitPriceUgx || input.unitPriceUgx < 0) throw new BadRequestException('unitPriceUgx must be >= 0');
  105: 
  106:     const totalRevenue = input.quantitySold * input.unitPriceUgx;
  107:     const costBasis = input.quantitySold * lot.unitCostUgx;
  108:     const grossMargin = totalRevenue - costBasis;
  109: 
  110:     const sale = await this.sales.create(
  111:       organizationDocument(context, {
  112:         lotId: lot._id,
  113:         saleCode: input.saleCode,
  114:         saleDate: new Date(input.saleDate),
  115:         quantitySold: input.quantitySold,
  116:         unitPriceUgx: input.unitPriceUgx,
  117:         totalRevenueUgx: totalRevenue,
  118:         costBasisUgx: costBasis,
  119:         grossMarginUgx: grossMargin,
  120:         buyerName: input.buyerName,
  121:         paymentStatus: input.paymentStatus,
  122:         createdBy: actor,
  123:       }),
  124:     );
  125: 
  126:     // Reduce lot quantity
  127:     const newQty = lot.quantity - input.quantitySold;
  128:     const newStatus = newQty === 0 ? 'sold_out' : 'partially_sold';
  129:     await this.lots.updateOne(
  130:       { _id: lot._id },
  131:       { $set: { quantity: newQty, status: newStatus } },
  132:     );
  133: 
  134:     // Post to GL
  135:     await this.gl.postSale(context, sale.toObject(), lot.toObject());
  136: 
  137:     return sale.toObject();
  138:   }
  139: 
  140:   async listLots(rawContext: OrganizationContext): Promise<HarvestLot[]> {
  141:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  142:     return this.lots.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ harvestDate: -1 }).lean();
  143:   }
  144: 
  145:   async listSales(rawContext: OrganizationContext): Promise<LotSale[]> {
  146:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  147:     return this.sales.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ saleDate: -1 }).lean();
  148:   }
  149: 
  150:   async listContracts(rawContext: OrganizationContext): Promise<GrowerContract[]> {
  151:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  152:     return this.contracts.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ startDate: -1 }).lean();
  153:   }
  154: 
  155:   async createContract(
  156:     rawContext: OrganizationContext,
  157:     input: Partial<GrowerContract>,
  158:     actor: string,
  159:   ): Promise<GrowerContract> {
  160:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  161:     const created = await this.contracts.create(
  162:       organizationDocument(context, { ...input, createdBy: actor }),
  163:     );
  164:     return created.toObject();
  165:   }
  166: }

    9: @Injectable()
   10: export class HarvestGlService {
   11:   private readonly logger = new Logger(HarvestGlService.name);
   12: 
   13:   constructor(
   14:     private readonly posting: PostingService,
   15:     @InjectModel(HarvestLot.name) private lots: Model<HarvestLot>,
   16:     @InjectModel(LotSale.name) private sales: Model<LotSale>,
   17:     @InjectModel(GrowerContract.name) private contracts: Model<GrowerContract>,
   18:   ) {}
   19: 
   20:   /** Post: harvest received from own farm â†’ inventory */
   21:   async postOwnFarmHarvest(
   22:     context: OrganizationContext,
   23:     lot: HarvestLot,
   24:     biologicalAssetAccountCode = '1300',   // Biological Assets: Growing Trees
   25:     inventoryAccountCode = '1220',         // Inventory: Timber
   26:   ): Promise<void> {
   27:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   28:     const totalCost = lot.originalQuantity * lot.unitCostUgx;
   29: 
   30:     const entry = await this.posting.post(ctx, {
   31:       date: new Date(lot.harvestDate),
   32:       description: `Harvest intake: ${lot.lotCode} from block ${lot.blockId}`,
   33:       sourceType: 'HarvestLot',
   34:       sourceId: String(lot._id),
   35:       createdBy: lot.createdBy,
   36:       lines: [
   37:         { accountCode: inventoryAccountCode, debit: totalCost, memo: `${lot.originalQuantity} ${lot.unit} @ ${lot.unitCostUgx}` },
   38:         { accountCode: biologicalAssetAccountCode, credit: totalCost, memo: `Harvest from ${lot.blockId}` },
   39:       ],
   40:     });
   41:     await this.lots.updateOne({ _id: lot._id }, { $set: { journalEntryId: entry._id } });
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   /** Post: harvest received from grower â†’ inventory + payable */
   45:   async postGrowerHarvest(
   46:     context: OrganizationContext,
   47:     lot: HarvestLot,
   48:     inventoryAccountCode = '1220',
   49:     payableAccountCode = '2000',   // Accounts Payable
   50:   ): Promise<void> {
   51:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   52:     const totalCost = lot.originalQuantity * lot.unitCostUgx;
   53: 
   54:     const entry = await this.posting.post(ctx, {
   55:       date: new Date(lot.harvestDate),
   56:       description: `Grower harvest intake: ${lot.lotCode} from ${lot.blockId}`,
   57:       sourceType: 'HarvestLot',
   58:       sourceId: String(lot._id),
   59:       createdBy: lot.createdBy,
   60:       lines: [
   61:         { accountCode: inventoryAccountCode, debit: totalCost, memo: `${lot.originalQuantity} ${lot.unit} @ ${lot.unitCostUgx}` },
   62:         { accountCode: payableAccountCode, credit: totalCost, memo: `Payable to grower for ${lot.lotCode}` },
   63:       ],
   64:     });
   65:     await this.lots.updateOne({ _id: lot._id }, { $set: { journalEntryId: entry._id } });
   66:   }
   67: 
   68:   /** Post: sale of a lot â†’ revenue + COGS */
   69:   async postSale(
   70:     context: OrganizationContext,
   71:     sale: LotSale,
   72:     lot: HarvestLot,
   73:   ): Promise<void> {
   74:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);
   75:     const inventoryAccountCode = '1220';
   76:     const revenueAccountCode = lot.commodityType === 'honey' ? '4010' : '4000';
   77:     const cogsAccountCode = lot.commodityType === 'honey' ? '5010' : '5000';
   78: 
   79:     // If paymentStatus is 'paid', debit Cash/Bank. If 'pending', debit AR.
   80:     const paymentAccountCode = sale.paymentStatus === 'paid' ? '1000' : '1100'; // Cash or AR
   81:     // In a real system you'd pull the payment method â€” for now default to AR if not paid
   82: 
   83:     const lines = [
   84:       { accountCode: paymentAccountCode, debit: sale.totalRevenueUgx, memo: `Sale ${sale.saleCode}${sale.buyerName ? ' to ' + sale.buyerName : ''}` },
   85:       { accountCode: revenueAccountCode, credit: sale.totalRevenueUgx, memo: `${sale.quantitySold} ${lot.unit} @ ${sale.unitPriceUgx}` },
   86:       { accountCode: cogsAccountCode, debit: sale.costBasisUgx, memo: `COGS for ${sale.saleCode}` },
   87:       { accountCode: inventoryAccountCode, credit: sale.costBasisUgx, memo: `Inventory reduction for ${sale.saleCode}` },
   88:     ];
   89: 
   90:     const entry = await this.posting.post(ctx, {
   91:       date: new Date(sale.saleDate),
   92:       description: `Lot sale: ${sale.saleCode} from ${lot.lotCode}`,
   93:       sourceType: 'LotSale',
   94:       sourceId: String(sale._id),
   95:       createdBy: sale.createdBy,
   96:       lines,
   97:     });
   98:     await this.sales.updateOne({ _id: sale._id }, { $set: { journalEntryId: entry._id } });
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   /** Post: advance payment to grower */
  102:   async postGrowerAdvance(
  103:     context: OrganizationContext,
  104:     contract: GrowerContract,
  105:     amount: number,
  106:     actor: string,
  107:   ): Promise<void> {
  108:     const ctx = requireOrganizationContext(context);

  111:       description: `Grower advance: ${contract.contractCode} â€” ${contract.growerName}`,
  112:       sourceType: 'GrowerAdvance',
  113:       sourceId: `${contract._id}:advance:${Date.now()}`,
  114:       createdBy: actor,
  115:       lines: [

  117:         { accountCode: '1010', credit: amount, memo: `Cash advance` },

    1: import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
    3: import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
    4: import { Hive } from './hive.schema';
    5: import { CreateHiveDto } from './dto';
    6: import { OrganizationContext, requireOrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    7: import { organizationDocument, organizationFilter } from '../organizations/organization-scope';
    8: 
    9: @Injectable()
   10: export class HivesService {
   11:   constructor(@InjectModel(Hive.name) private model: Model<Hive>) {}
   12: 
   13:   findAll(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   14:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   15:     return this.model.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ hiveId: 1 }).lean();
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   async create(dto: CreateHiveDto, rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   19:   const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   20:   const payload = organizationDocument(context, dto as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
   21:   try {
   22:     return await this.model.create(payload);
   23:   } catch (e: any) {
   24:     if (e?.code === 11000) {
   25:       throw new ConflictException(
   26:         `Hive "${(dto as any).hiveId}" already exists in this organization`,
   27:       );
   28:     }
   29:     throw e;
   30:   }
   31: }
   32: 
   33:   async update(id: string, dto: Partial<CreateHiveDto>, rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   34:   const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   35:   if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid hive id');
   36:   const safeDto: any = { ...dto };
   37:   delete safeDto.organizationId;
   38:   delete safeDto.createdBy;
   39:   const x = await this.model.findOneAndUpdate(
   40:     organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }),
   41:     safeDto,
   42:     { new: true, runValidators: true },
   43:   ).lean();
   44:   if (!x) throw new NotFoundException('Hive not found');
   45:   return x;
   46: }
   47: }

   31:       user.role === 'owner' ? {} : { assignedTo: user.email.toLowerCase() };

   39:         ? { ...r, status: 'Overdue' }
   40:         : r,
   41:     );

   25:  async create(b:any,actor=''){
   26:   if(!eventTypes.includes(b?.eventType))throw new BadRequestException('Invalid eventType');

   28:   const quantity=positive(b.quantity,'quantity');
   29:   const movementDate=validDate(b.movementDate);

   29:   async dashboard(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   30:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   31:     const itemFilter = organizationFilter(context);
   32:     const movementFilter = organizationFilter(context);
   33:     const [items, moves, movementCount] = await Promise.all([
   34:       this.items.find(itemFilter).lean(),
   35:       this.moves.find(movementFilter).sort({ createdAt: -1 }).limit(10).lean(),
   36:       this.moves.countDocuments(movementFilter),
   37:     ]);
   38:     const low = items.filter(i => i.quantityOnHand <= i.reorderLevel);
   39:     return {
   40:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
   41:       totals: {
   42:         items: items.length,
   43:         lowStock: low.length,
   44:         stockValueUgx: items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.quantityOnHand * i.averageUnitCostUgx, 0),
   45:         movementCount,
   46:       },
   47:       lowStock: low,
   48:       recentMovements: moves,
   49:     };
   50:   }

   52:   async listItems(rawContext: OrganizationContext, q: any = {}) {
   53:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   54:     const f: any = organizationFilter(context);
   55:     if (q.category && categories.includes(q.category)) f.category = q.category;
   56:     if (q.lowStock === 'true') f.$expr = { $lte: ['$quantityOnHand', '$reorderLevel'] };
   57:     return this.items.find(f).sort({ name: 1 }).lean();
   58:   }

   60:   async createItem(rawContext: OrganizationContext, b: any, actor = '') {
   61:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   62:     if (!b?.itemCode?.trim() || !b?.name?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('itemCode and name are required');
   63:     if (!categories.includes(b.category)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid category');
   64:     const quantity = num(b.quantityOnHand ?? 0, 'quantityOnHand');
   65:     const cost = num(b.averageUnitCostUgx ?? 0, 'averageUnitCostUgx');
   66:     const payload = organizationDocument(context, {
   67:       itemCode: b.itemCode.trim(),
   68:       name: b.name.trim(),
   69:       category: b.category,
   70:       unit: b.unit || 'piece',
   71:       quantityOnHand: 0,
   72:       reorderLevel: num(b.reorderLevel ?? 0, 'reorderLevel'),
   73:       averageUnitCostUgx: cost,
   74:       storageLocation: b.storageLocation || '',
   75:       supplier: b.supplier || '',
   76:       linkedAssetCode: b.linkedAssetCode || '',
   77:       notes: b.notes || '',
   78:     });
   79:     const item = await this.items.create({ ...payload, createdBy: actor || context.userId });
   80:     if (quantity > 0) {
   81:       await this.postMovement(context, {
   82:         itemId: String(item._id),
   83:         movementCode: `OPEN-${item.itemCode}`,
   84:         movementType: 'opening_balance',
   85:         movementDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
   86:         quantity,
   87:         unitCostUgx: cost,
   88:         referenceType: 'opening_balance',
   89:         referenceCode: item.itemCode,
   90:         reason: 'Initial stock entry',
   91:       }, actor);
   92:     }
   93:     return this.items.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: item._id })).lean();
   94:   }
   95: 
   96:   async updateItem(rawContext: OrganizationContext, id: string, b: any) {
   97:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   98:     const item: any = await this.items.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }));
   99:     if (!item) throw new NotFoundException('Stock item not found');
  100:     for (const k of ['name', 'unit', 'storageLocation', 'supplier', 'linkedAssetCode', 'notes'] as const) {
  101:       if (b[k] !== undefined) item[k] = b[k];
  102:     }
  103:     if (b.category !== undefined) {
  104:       if (!categories.includes(b.category)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid category');
  105:       item.category = b.category;
  106:     }
  107:     if (b.reorderLevel !== undefined) item.reorderLevel = num(b.reorderLevel, 'reorderLevel');
  108:     if (b.averageUnitCostUgx !== undefined) item.averageUnitCostUgx = num(b.averageUnitCostUgx, 'averageUnitCostUgx');
  109:     await item.save();
  110:     return item;
  111:   }
  112: 
  113:   async listMovements(rawContext: OrganizationContext, itemId?: string) {
  114:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  115:     const filter: any = organizationFilter(context);
  116:     if (itemId) {
  117:       const parent = await this.items.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: itemId })).select({ _id: 1 }).lean();
  118:       if (!parent) throw new NotFoundException('Stock item not found');
  119:       filter.itemId = itemId;
  120:     }
  121:     return this.moves.find(filter).sort({ movementDate: -1, createdAt: -1 }).limit(500).lean();
  122:   }
  123: 
  124:   async findMovementByCode(rawContext: OrganizationContext, code: string) {
  125:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  126:     return this.moves.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { movementCode: code })).lean();
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   async postMovement(rawContext: OrganizationContext, b: any, actor = '') {
  130:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
  131:     if (!b?.itemId || !b?.movementCode?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('itemId and movementCode are required');
  132:     if (!types.includes(b.movementType)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid movementType');
  133:     const item: any = await this.items.findOne(organizationFilter(context, { _id: b.itemId }));
  134:     if (!item) throw new NotFoundException('Stock item not found');
  135:     const quantity = num(b.quantity, 'quantity', 0.001);
  136:     const unitCost = num(b.unitCostUgx ?? item.averageUnitCostUgx, 'unitCostUgx');
  137:     const movementDate = date(b.movementDate);
  138:     let delta = quantity;
  139:     if (['issue', 'adjustment_decrease', 'write_off'].includes(b.movementType)) delta = -quantity;
  140:     const next = item.quantityOnHand + delta;
  141:     if (next < 0) throw new BadRequestException(`Insufficient stock: available ${item.quantityOnHand} ${item.unit}`);
  142:     const prior = item.quantityOnHand;
  143:     const priorCost = item.averageUnitCostUgx;
  144:     if (['receipt', 'return', 'opening_balance', 'adjustment_increase'].includes(b.movementType) && next > 0) {
  145:       item.averageUnitCostUgx = prior + quantity > 0
  146:         ? ((prior * priorCost) + (quantity * unitCost)) / (prior + quantity)
  147:         : unitCost;
  148:     }
  149:     item.quantityOnHand = next;
  150:     await item.save();
  151:     try {
  152:       return await this.moves.create(organizationDocument(context, {
  153:         itemId: String(item._id),
  154:         movementCode: b.movementCode.trim(),
  155:         movementType: b.movementType,
  156:         movementDate,
  157:         quantity,
  158:         unitCostUgx: unitCost,
  159:         balanceAfter: next,
  160:         referenceType: b.referenceType || '',
  161:         referenceCode: b.referenceCode || '',
  162:         destinationOrSource: b.destinationOrSource || '',
  163:         reason: b.reason || '',
  164:         notes: b.notes || '',
  165:       }));
  166:     } catch (e) {
  167:       item.quantityOnHand = prior;
  168:       item.averageUnitCostUgx = priorCost;
  169:       await item.save();
  170:       throw e;
  171:     }
  172:   }
  173: }

   42:     if (!assetTypes.includes(b.assetType)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid assetType');

   73:       await this.gl.postAssetPurchase(context, asset.toObject());
   74:     } catch (e: any) {
   75:       if (e?.status === 400 && /locked period/i.test(e.message)) {
   76:         await this.assets.findByIdAndDelete(asset._id);
   77:         throw e;
   78:       }

   80:     }

  169:       }

  175:   async updateRecord(id: string, b: any, actor: string, organizationId: string) {
  176:     const record = await this.records.findOne({ _id: id, organizationId });
  177:     if (!record) throw new NotFoundException('Maintenance record not found');
  178: 
  179:     const previousStatus = record.status;
  180:     if (b.status !== undefined) {
  181:       if (!maintenanceStatuses.includes(b.status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid status');
  182:       record.status = b.status;

  204:       }

  236:     // Post initial stock if quantity > 0 and unitCost > 0
  237:     try {
  238:       const context: OrganizationContext = { organizationId, userId: actor, roles: [] };
  239:       await this.gl.postPartStockIncrease(context, part.toObject(), part.quantityOnHand, actor);
  240:     } catch (e: any) {
  241:       this.logger.error(`GL post failed for part ${part.partCode}: ${e.message}`, e.stack);
  242:     }
  243: 
  244:     return part;
  245:   }
  246: 
  247:   async adjustPart(id: string, b: any, actor: string, organizationId: string) {
  248:     const p = await this.parts.findOne({ _id: id, organizationId });
  249:     if (!p) throw new NotFoundException('Spare part not found');
  250:     const previousQty = p.quantityOnHand;
  251: 
  252:     if (b.quantityOnHand !== undefined) p.quantityOnHand = num(b.quantityOnHand, 'quantityOnHand');
  253:     if (b.reorderLevel !== undefined) p.reorderLevel = num(b.reorderLevel, 'reorderLevel');
  254:     if (b.unitCostUgx !== undefined) p.unitCostUgx = num(b.unitCostUgx, 'unitCostUgx');
  255:     for (const k of ['name','category','unit','storageLocation','supplier','notes'] as const) {
  256:       if (b[k] !== undefined) (p as any)[k] = b[k];
  257:     }
  258:     await p.save();
  259: 
  260:     // Post stock increase
  261:     const increase = p.quantityOnHand - previousQty;
  262:     if (increase > 0) {
  263:       try {
  264:         const context: OrganizationContext = { organizationId, userId: actor, roles: [] };
  265:         await this.gl.postPartStockIncrease(context, p.toObject(), increase, actor);
  266:       } catch (e: any) {
  267:         this.logger.error(`GL post failed for part adjust ${p.partCode}: ${e.message}`, e.stack);
  268:       }
  269:     }
  270: 
  271:     return p;
  272:   }
  273: 
  274:   // dashboard() â€” kept as-is, needs organizationId now
  275:   async dashboard(organizationId: string) {
  276:     const f = { organizationId };
  277:     const [assets, records, parts] = await Promise.all([
  278:       this.assets.find(f).lean(),
  279:       this.records.find(f).lean(),
  280:       this.parts.find(f).lean(),
  281:     ]);
  282:     const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  283:     const completedCost = records
  284:       .filter((r: any) => r.status === 'completed')
  285:       .reduce((s: number, r: any) => s + (r.labourCostUgx || 0) + (r.partsCostUgx || 0) + (r.otherCostUgx || 0), 0);
  286:     return {
  287:       totals: {
  288:         assets: assets.length,
  289:         activeAssets: assets.filter((a: any) => a.status === 'active').length,
  290:         needsRepair: assets.filter((a: any) => a.status === 'needs_repair').length,
  291:         overdue: records.filter((r: any) => r.status === 'planned' && r.plannedDate < today).length,
  292:         serviceOverdue: assets.filter((a: any) => a.nextServiceDate && a.nextServiceDate < today).length,
  293:         lowStock: parts.filter((p: any) => p.quantityOnHand <= p.reorderLevel).length,
  294:         completedMaintenanceCostUgx: completedCost,
  295:       },
  296:     };
  297:   }
  298: }

   10: 
   11:   /** Capitalise an asset purchase â€” Dr PP&E or Tools / Cr Cash */
   12:   async postAssetPurchase(
   13:     context: OrganizationContext,
   14:     asset: any,
   15:   ): Promise<void> {
   16:     if (!asset.purchaseCostUgx || asset.purchaseCostUgx <= 0) return;
   17: 
   18:     const debitAccount = this.accountForAssetType(asset.assetType);
   19: 
   20:     await this.posting.post(context, {
   21:       date: asset.purchaseDate ? new Date(asset.purchaseDate) : new Date(),
   22:       description: `Asset purchase: ${asset.assetCode} â€” ${asset.name}`,
   23:       sourceType: 'FarmAsset',
   24:       sourceId: String(asset._id),
   25:       createdBy: asset.createdBy,
   26:       lines: [
   27:         { accountCode: debitAccount, debit: asset.purchaseCostUgx, memo: `${asset.name} (${asset.assetType})` },
   28:         { accountCode: '1000', credit: asset.purchaseCostUgx, memo: `Payment for ${asset.assetCode}` },
   29:       ],
   30:     });
   31: 
   32:     this.logger.log(`Posted asset purchase GL: ${asset.assetCode} ${asset.purchaseCostUgx} UGX`);
   33:   }
   34: 
   35:   /** Complete a maintenance record â€” Dr Repair Expense / Cr Cash */
   36:   async postMaintenanceCompletion(
   37:     context: OrganizationContext,
   38:     record: any,
   39:     asset: any,
   40:   ): Promise<void> {
   41:     const total =
   42:       (record.labourCostUgx || 0) +
   43:       (record.partsCostUgx || 0) +
   44:       (record.otherCostUgx || 0);
   45: 
   46:     if (total <= 0) return;
   47: 
   48:     await this.posting.post(context, {
   49:       date: record.completedDate ? new Date(record.completedDate) : new Date(),
   50:       description: `Maintenance: ${record.maintenanceCode} â€” ${record.title}`,
   51:       sourceType: 'MaintenanceRecord',
   52:       sourceId: String(record._id),
   53:       createdBy: record.createdBy,
   54:       lines: [
   55:         { accountCode: '6220', debit: total, memo: `${record.maintenanceType}: ${asset?.name ?? record.assetId}` },
   56:         { accountCode: '1000', credit: total, memo: `Maintenance payment for ${record.maintenanceCode}` },
   57:       ],
   58:     });
   59: 
   60:     this.logger.log(`Posted maintenance GL: ${record.maintenanceCode} ${total} UGX`);
   61:   }
   62: 
   63:   /** Spare part stock increase â€” Dr Inventory: Supplies / Cr Cash */
   64:   async postPartStockIncrease(
   65:     context: OrganizationContext,
   66:     part: any,
   67:     quantityIncrease: number,
   68:     actor: string,
   69:   ): Promise<void> {
   70:     if (quantityIncrease <= 0) return;
   71:     const value = quantityIncrease * (part.unitCostUgx || 0);
   72:     if (value <= 0) return;
   73: 
   74:     await this.posting.post(context, {
   75:       date: new Date(),
   76:       description: `Spare part stock increase: ${part.partCode} +${quantityIncrease} ${part.unit}`,
   77:       sourceType: 'SparePartAdjustment',
   78:       sourceId: `${part._id}:adjustment:${Date.now()}`,
   79:       createdBy: actor,
   80:       lines: [
   81:         { accountCode: '1230', debit: value, memo: `${part.name} +${quantityIncrease}` },
   82:         { accountCode: '1000', credit: value, memo: `Purchase of ${part.partCode}` },
   83:       ],
   84:     });
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   private accountForAssetType(assetType: string): string {
   88:     switch (assetType) {
   89:       case 'hand_tool':
   90:       case 'power_tool':
   91:       case 'safety_equipment':
   92:         return '6200';   // Tools & Equipment (expense)
   93:       case 'vehicle':
   94:       case 'water_system':
   95:       case 'building':
   96:       case 'apiary_equipment':
   97:       case 'other':
   98:       default:
   99:         return '1400';   // PPE: Equipment (asset)
  100:     }
  101:   }

   10:   OrganizationMembership,
   11:   OrganizationMembershipDocument,
   12: } from './organization-membership.schema';
   13: import { OrganizationContext } from './organization-context';
   14: 
   15: @Injectable()
   16: export class OrganizationAccessService {
   17:   constructor(
   18:     @InjectModel(Organization.name)
   19:     private readonly organizations: Model<OrganizationDocument>,
   20:     @InjectModel(OrganizationMembership.name)
   21:     private readonly memberships: Model<OrganizationMembershipDocument>,
   22:   ) {}
   23: 
   24:   /**
   25:    * Resolve a verified active organization context for a user.
   26:    * The organization ID is only a selector: membership and organization status
   27:    * are checked against the database on every call.
   28:    */
   29:   async resolveContext(userId: string, requestedOrganizationId: string): Promise<OrganizationContext> {
   30:     if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(userId) || !Types.ObjectId.isValid(requestedOrganizationId)) {
   31:       throw new NotFoundException('Organization or membership not found');
   32:     }
   33: 
   34:     const organization = await this.organizations.findOne({
   35:       _id: new Types.ObjectId(requestedOrganizationId),
   36:       status: 'active',
   37:     }).lean();
   38: 
   39:     if (!organization) {
   40:       throw new NotFoundException('Organization or membership not found');
   41:     }
   42: 
   43:     // Membership schema stores both references as strings, so query with the
   44:     // canonical string IDs rather than BSON ObjectId values.
   45:     const membership = await this.memberships.findOne({
   46:       userId: String(userId),
   47:       organizationId: String(requestedOrganizationId),
   48:       status: 'active',
   49:     }).lean();
   50: 
   51:     if (!membership) {
   52:       throw new ForbiddenException('No active membership for the selected organization');
   53:     }
   54: 
   55:     return {
   56:       organizationId: String(organization._id),
   57:       userId: String(userId),
   58:       roles: Array.isArray((membership as any).roles)
   59:         ? (membership as any).roles
   60:         : (typeof (membership as any).role === 'string' && (membership as any).role
   61:             ? [(membership as any).role]
   62:             : []),
   63:     };
   64:   }
   65: 
   66:   async listMyOrganizations(userId: string) {
   67:     if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(userId)) return [];
   68:     const memberships = await this.memberships.find({
   69:       userId: String(userId),
   70:       status: 'active',
   71:     }).lean();
   72: 
   73:     const organizationIds = memberships
   74:       .map((m: any) => String(m.organizationId))
   75:       .filter((id: string) => Types.ObjectId.isValid(id));
   76:     if (!organizationIds.length) return [];
   77: 
   78:     const organizations = await this.organizations.find({
   79:       _id: { $in: organizationIds.map((id: string) => new Types.ObjectId(id)) },
   80:       status: 'active',
   81:     }).lean();
   82: 
   83:     const byId = new Map(organizations.map((o: any) => [String(o._id), o]));
   84:     return memberships.flatMap((m: any) => {
   85:       const org = byId.get(String(m.organizationId));
   86:       return org ? [{
   87:         organizationId: String(org._id),
   88:         slug: org.slug,
   89:         name: org.name,
   90:         roles: Array.isArray(m.roles)
   91:           ? m.roles
   92:           : (typeof m.role === 'string' && m.role ? [m.role] : []),
   93:       }] : [];
   94:     });
   95:   }
   96: }

    1: /**
    2:  * Phase 86 â€” request-scoped organization context primitives.
    3:  *
    4:  * Security note:
    5:  * These primitives do not themselves establish tenant isolation. They provide
    6:  * a typed, fail-closed boundary for modules to adopt. Never trust an
    7:  * organization ID supplied by a query string/body/header without resolving
    8:  * the authenticated user's active membership server-side.
    9:  */
   10: export interface OrganizationContext {
   11:   organizationId: string;
   12:   userId: string;
   13:   roles: string[];
   14: }
   15: 
   16: export const ORGANIZATION_CONTEXT_KEY = 'forestOS.organizationContext';
   17: 
   18: export class OrganizationContextError extends Error {
   19:   constructor(message = 'An active organization context is required') {
   20:     super(message);
   21:     this.name = 'OrganizationContextError';
   22:   }
   23: }
   24: 
   25: export function requireOrganizationContext(
   26:   context: OrganizationContext | undefined | null,
   27: ): OrganizationContext {
   28:   if (!context || !context.organizationId || !context.userId || !Array.isArray(context.roles)) {
   29:     throw new OrganizationContextError();
   30:   }
   31:   return context;
   32: }
   33: 
   34: export function hasOrganizationRole(
   35:   context: OrganizationContext,
   36:   allowedRoles: string[],
   37: ): boolean {
   38:   return context.roles.some((role) => allowedRoles.includes(role));
   39: }

   72:   async addMembership(b: any, actor: string) {

  145:       .find({ organizationId: id })
  146:       .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
  147:       .limit(Math.max(1, Math.min(500, Math.floor(Number(limit) || 100))))
  148:       .lean();

    1: /**
    2:  * Helpers for consistently applying organization scoping to Mongo queries.
    3:  *
    4:  * Adoption guidance:
    5:  * - Use organizationFilter(context, filters) for reads and updates.
    6:  * - Use organizationDocument(context, payload) for creates.
    7:  * - Do not spread user-provided objects after organizationId; that could
    8:  *   override the trusted tenant ID.
    9:  * - These helpers are not a substitute for membership resolution or tests.
   10:  */
   11: import {
   12:   OrganizationContext,
   13:   requireOrganizationContext,
   14: } from './organization-context';
   15: 
   16: export type OrganizationFilter = Record<string, unknown> & { organizationId: string };
   17: 
   18: export function organizationFilter(
   19:   rawContext: OrganizationContext | undefined | null,
   20:   filters: Record<string, unknown> = {},
   21: ): OrganizationFilter {
   22:   const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   23:   const { organizationId: _untrustedOrganizationId, ...safeFilters } = filters;
   24:   return { ...safeFilters, organizationId: context.organizationId };
   25: }
   26: 
   27: export function organizationDocument<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
   28:   rawContext: OrganizationContext | undefined | null,
   29:   payload: T,
   30: ): T & { organizationId: string; createdBy: string } {
   31:   const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   32:   const { organizationId: _untrustedOrganizationId, createdBy: _untrustedCreatedBy, ...safePayload } = payload;
   33:   return {
   34:     ...safePayload,
   35:     organizationId: context.organizationId,
   36:     createdBy: context.userId,
   37:   } as T & { organizationId: string; createdBy: string };
   38: }

   19:   findAll(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {

   22:   }
   23: 
   24:   async create(dto: CreatePlantationDto, rawContext: OrganizationContext, actorEmail?: string) {
   25:   const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   26:   const payload = organizationDocument(context, dto as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
   27:   let row: any;
   28:   try {
   29:     row = await this.model.create(payload);
   30:   } catch (e: any) {
   31:     if (e?.code === 11000) {
   32:       throw new ConflictException(`Plantation block "${dto.blockId}" already exists in this organization`);
   33:     }
   34:     throw e;
   35:   }
   36:   if (actorEmail) {
   37:     await this.audit.recordForTenant(
   38:       { tenantId: context.organizationId, actorEmail },
   39:       'created', 'plantation', String(row._id),
   40:       `Created plantation block ${row.blockId}`, { organizationId: context.organizationId },
   41:     );
   42:   }
   43:   return row;
   44: }
   45: 
   46:   async update(id: string, dto: Partial<CreatePlantationDto>, rawContext: OrganizationContext, actorEmail?: string) {
   47:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   48:     if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid plantation id');
   49:     const safeDto: any = { ...dto };
   50:     delete safeDto.organizationId;
   51:     delete safeDto.createdBy;
   52:     const x: any = await this.model.findOneAndUpdate(
   53:       organizationFilter(context, { _id: id }),
   54:       safeDto,
   55:       { new: true, runValidators: true },
   56:     ).lean();
   57:     if (!x) throw new NotFoundException('Plantation block not found');
   58:     if (actorEmail) {
   59:       await this.audit.recordForTenant(
   60:         { tenantId: context.organizationId, actorEmail },
   61:         'updated', 'plantation', String(x._id),
   62:         `Updated plantation block ${x.blockId}`, {
   63:           organizationId: context.organizationId,
   64:           fields: Object.keys(safeDto),
   65:         },
   66:       );
   67:     }
   68:     return x;
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   async intelligence(rawContext: OrganizationContext) {
   72:     const context = requireOrganizationContext(rawContext);
   73:     const blocks: any[] = await this.model.find(organizationFilter(context)).sort({ blockId: 1 }).lean();
   74:     const result = [];
   75:     for (const b of blocks) {
   76:       // Growth samples are joined only for block IDs visible in this organization.
   77:       const samples: any[] = await this.growth.find(organizationFilter(context, { blockId: b.blockId })).sort({ sampleDate: 1 }).lean();
   78:       const latest = samples[samples.length - 1];
   79:       const survival = (b.initialTrees && b.latestLiveTrees !== undefined)
   80:         ? Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(b.latestLiveTrees) / Number(b.initialTrees) * 100))
   81:         : null;
   82:       result.push({
   83:         ...b,
   84:         survivalRatePercent: survival,
   85:         treeLossCount: b.initialTrees !== undefined && b.latestLiveTrees !== undefined
   86:           ? Math.max(0, b.initialTrees - b.latestLiveTrees) : null,
   87:         growthSampleCount: samples.length,
   88:         latestGrowthSample: latest ? {
   89:           sampleDate: latest.sampleDate,
   90:           averageHeightM: latest.averageHeightM,
   91:           averageDbhCm: latest.averageDbhCm,
   92:           treeCount: latest.treeCount,
   93:         } : null,
   94:         measurementNote: 'Survival rate uses recorded initial and live tree counts only; growth is a sample measurement, not a timber volume estimate.',
   95:       });
   96:     }
   97:     return {
   98:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   99:       organizationId: context.organizationId,
  100:       blocks: result,
  101:       totals: {
  102:         blocks: result.length,
  103:         areaAcres: result.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.areaAcres || 0), 0),
  104:         initialTreesRecorded: result.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.initialTrees || 0), 0),
  105:         liveTreesRecorded: result.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.latestLiveTrees || 0), 0),
  106:         blocksMissingTreeCounts: result.filter(b => b.initialTrees === undefined || b.latestLiveTrees === undefined).length,
  107:       },
  108:       warnings: [
  109:         'Counts and areas are only as reliable as field records.',
  110:         'Do not use sample height/DBH alone to estimate saleable timber without a validated forestry method.',
  111:       ],
  112:     };
  113:   }
  114: }

   21: function num(v: any, n: string, min = 0) {
   22:   const x = Number(v);
   23:   if (!Number.isFinite(x) || x < min) throw new BadRequestException(`${n} must be a number >= ${min}`);
   24:   return x;
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function date(v: any, n: string, required: true): string;
   28: function date(v: any, n: string, required: false): string | null;
   29: function date(v: any, n: string, required = true): string | null {
   30:   if (v === '' || v === undefined || v === null) {
   31:     if (!required) return null;
   32:     throw new BadRequestException(`${n} is required`);
   33:   }
   34:   if (typeof v !== 'string' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v))
   35:     throw new BadRequestException(`${n} must be YYYY-MM-DD`);
   36:   const [year, month, day] = v.split('-').map(Number);
   37:   const parsed = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
   38:   if (
   39:     Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ||
   40:     parsed.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
   41:     parsed.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
   42:     parsed.getUTCDate() !== day
   43:   ) throw new BadRequestException(`${n} must be a valid calendar date`);
   44:   return v;
   45: }

   61:   async dashboard(organizationId: string) {
   62:     const f = { organizationId };
   63:     const [suppliers, requests, orders, lines, receipts] = await Promise.all([
   64:       this.suppliers.find(f).lean(),
   65:       this.requests.find(f).lean(),
   66:       this.orders.find(f).lean(),
   67:       this.lines.find(f).lean(),
   68:       this.receipts.find(f).lean(),

   71:     const activeOrders = orders.filter(o => !['received', 'cancelled'].includes(o.status));
   72:     const orderValue = orders
   73:       .filter(o => !['cancelled'].includes(o.status))
   74:       .reduce((sum, o) =>
   75:         sum +
   76:         lines.filter(l => l.orderId === String(o._id))
   77:           .reduce((s, l) => s + l.quantityOrdered * l.unitPriceUgx, 0) +
   78:         (o.transportCostUgx || 0) +
   79:         (o.otherCostUgx || 0), 0);
   80:     const overdue = activeOrders.filter(o => o.expectedDate < today);
   81:     return {
   82:       totals: {
   83:         suppliers: suppliers.filter(s => s.status === 'active').length,
   84:         pendingRequests: requests.filter(r => ['submitted', 'approved'].includes(r.status)).length,
   85:         openOrders: activeOrders.length,
   86:         overdueOrders: overdue.length,
   87:         receipts: receipts.length,
   88:         committedOrderValueUgx: orderValue,
   89:       },
   90:       overdueOrders: overdue.slice(0, 10),
   91:       recentOrders: orders.sort((a, b) => String(b.orderDate).localeCompare(String(a.orderDate))).slice(0, 10),

  202:   async createOrder(b: any, actor: string, organizationId: string): Promise<any> {
  203:     if (!organizationId) throw new BadRequestException('X-Organization-Id required');
  204:     if (!b?.orderCode?.trim() || !b?.supplierId)
  205:       throw new BadRequestException('orderCode and supplierId are required');
  206:     const supplier = await this.suppliers.findOne({ _id: b.supplierId, organizationId });
  207:     if (!supplier) throw new NotFoundException('Supplier not found');
  208:     if (supplier.status !== 'active') throw new BadRequestException('Supplier is not active');
  209:     const orderDate = date(b.orderDate, 'orderDate', true);
  210:     const expectedDate = date(b.expectedDate, 'expectedDate', true);
  211:     if (expectedDate < orderDate)
  212:       throw new BadRequestException('Expected date cannot be before order date');
  213:     const items = Array.isArray(b.lines) ? b.lines : [];
  214:     if (!items.length) throw new BadRequestException('At least one order line is required');
  215:     if (b.requestId) {
  216:       const req = await this.requests.findOne({ _id: b.requestId, organizationId });
  217:       if (!req) throw new NotFoundException('Purchase request not found');
  218:       if (!['approved', 'submitted'].includes(req.status))
  219:         throw new BadRequestException('Request must be submitted or approved before conversion');
  220:     }
  221:     const order = await this.orders.create({
  222:       organizationId,
  223:       orderCode: b.orderCode.trim(),
  224:       supplierId: b.supplierId,
  225:       requestId: b.requestId || null,
  226:       orderDate,
  227:       expectedDate,
  228:       status: b.status || 'draft',
  229:       currency: 'UGX',
  230:       transportCostUgx: num(b.transportCostUgx ?? 0, 'transportCostUgx'),
  231:       otherCostUgx: num(b.otherCostUgx ?? 0, 'otherCostUgx'),
  232:       terms: b.terms || '',
  233:       notes: b.notes || '',
  234:       createdBy: actor,
  235:     });
  236:     try {
  237:       for (const l of items) {
  238:         if (!l?.itemName?.trim()) throw new BadRequestException('Every line needs an itemName');
  239:         await this.lines.create({
  240:           organizationId,
  241:           orderId: String(order._id),
  242:           itemName: l.itemName.trim(),
  243:           itemCode: l.itemCode || '',
  244:           quantityOrdered: num(l.quantityOrdered, 'quantityOrdered', 0.001),
  245:           quantityReceived: 0,
  246:           unitPriceUgx: num(l.unitPriceUgx, 'unitPriceUgx'),
  247:           unit: l.unit || 'piece',
  248:           notes: l.notes || '',
  249:         });
  250:       }
  251:       if (b.requestId) await this.requests.findByIdAndUpdate(b.requestId, { $set: { status: 'converted' } });
  252:     } catch (e) {
  253:       await this.orders.findByIdAndDelete(order._id);
  254:       await this.lines.deleteMany({ orderId: String(order._id) });
  255:       throw e;
  256:     }
  257:     return (await this.listOrders(organizationId)).find(o => String(o._id) === String(order._id));
  258:   }
  259: 
  260:   async updateOrder(id: string, b: any, organizationId: string) {
  261:     const o = await this.orders.findOne({ _id: id, organizationId });
  262:     if (!o) throw new NotFoundException('Purchase order not found');
  263:     if (b.status !== undefined) {
  264:       if (!orderStatuses.includes(b.status)) throw new BadRequestException('Invalid order status');
  265:       if (b.status === 'received') {
  266:         const ls = await this.lines.find({ orderId: id, organizationId }).lean();
  267:         if (ls.some(l => l.quantityReceived < l.quantityOrdered))
  268:           throw new BadRequestException('Cannot mark received while ordered quantities remain outstanding');
  269:       }
  270:       o.status = b.status;
  271:     }
  272:     if (b.expectedDate !== undefined) o.expectedDate = date(b.expectedDate, 'expectedDate', true);
  273:     if (b.notes !== undefined) o.notes = b.notes;
  274:     await o.save();
  275:     return o;
  276:   }
  277: 
  278:   async listReceipts(orderId: string | undefined, organizationId: string): Promise<any[]> {
  279:     const f: any = { organizationId };
  280:     if (orderId) f.orderId = orderId;
  281:     const [r, ls] = await Promise.all([
  282:       this.receipts.find(f).sort({ receivedDate: -1 }).lean(),
  283:       this.receiptLines.find({ organizationId }).lean(),
  284:     ]);
  285:     return r.map(x => ({ ...x, lines: ls.filter(l => l.receiptId === String(x._id)) }));
  286:   }
  287: 
  288:   async createReceipt(b: any, actor: string, organizationId: string): Promise<any> {
  289:     if (!organizationId) throw new BadRequestException('X-Organization-Id required');
  290:     if (!b?.orderId || !b?.receiptCode?.trim())
  291:       throw new BadRequestException('orderId and receiptCode are required');
  292:     const order = await this.orders.findOne({ _id: b.orderId, organizationId });
  293:     if (!order) throw new NotFoundException('Purchase order not found');
  294:     if (['cancelled', 'received'].includes(order.status))
  295:       throw new BadRequestException('Cannot receive against a cancelled or fully received order');
  296:     const receivedDate = date(b.receivedDate, 'receivedDate', true);
  297:     const items = Array.isArray(b.lines) ? b.lines : [];
  298:     if (!items.length) throw new BadRequestException('At least one receipt line is required');
  299: 
  300:     const receipt = await this.receipts.create({
  301:       organizationId,
  302:       orderId: b.orderId,
  303:       receiptCode: b.receiptCode.trim(),
  304:       receivedDate,
  305:       status: b.status || 'received',
  306:       receivedBy: b.receivedBy || '',
  307:       deliveryNoteReference: b.deliveryNoteReference || '',
  308:       notes: b.notes || '',
  309:       createdBy: actor,
  310:     });
  311: 
  312:     const createdReceiptLines: any[] = [];
  313:     const touchedOrderLines: any[] = [];
  314:     try {
  315:       for (const item of items) {
  316:         const line = await this.lines.findOne({ _id: item.orderLineId, orderId: b.orderId, organizationId });
  317:         if (!line) throw new BadRequestException('Receipt line must reference a line on this purchase order');
  318:         const qty = num(item.quantityReceived, 'quantityReceived', 0.001);
  319:         if (line.quantityReceived + qty > line.quantityOrdered)
  320:           throw new BadRequestException(`Receipt exceeds outstanding quantity for ${line.itemName}`);
  321:         const rl = await this.receiptLines.create({
  322:           organizationId,
  323:           receiptId: String(receipt._id),
  324:           orderLineId: String(line._id),
  325:           quantityReceived: qty,
  326:           condition: item.condition || '',
  327:           notes: item.notes || '',
  328:         });
  329:         createdReceiptLines.push(rl.toObject());
  330:         const prevQty = line.quantityReceived;
  331:         line.quantityReceived += qty;
  332:         await line.save();
  333:         touchedOrderLines.push({ lineId: String(line._id), prevQty });
  334:       }
  335:       const ls = await this.lines.find({ orderId: b.orderId, organizationId }).lean();
  336:       const allReceived = ls.every(l => l.quantityReceived >= l.quantityOrdered);
  337:       const anyReceived = ls.some(l => l.quantityReceived > 0);
  338:       order.status = allReceived ? 'received' : anyReceived ? 'part_received' : 'approved';
  339:       await order.save();
  340:     } catch (e) {
  341:       await this.receipts.findByIdAndDelete(receipt._id);
  342:       await this.receiptLines.deleteMany({ receiptId: String(receipt._id) });
  343:       for (const t of touchedOrderLines) {
  344:         await this.lines.updateOne({ _id: t.lineId }, { $set: { quantityReceived: t.prevQty } });
  345:       }
  346:       throw e;
  347:     }
  348: 
  349:     // Post to GL
  350:     try {
  351:       const context: OrganizationContext = { organizationId, userId: actor, roles: [] };
  352:       const orderLines = await this.lines.find({ orderId: b.orderId, organizationId }).lean();
  353:       await this.gl.postReceipt(context, receipt.toObject(), createdReceiptLines, orderLines);
  354:     } catch (e: any) {
  355:       if (e?.status === 400 && /locked period/i.test(e.message)) {
  356:         await this.receipts.findByIdAndDelete(receipt._id);
  357:         await this.receiptLines.deleteMany({ receiptId: String(receipt._id) });
  358:         for (const t of touchedOrderLines) {
  359:           await this.lines.updateOne({ _id: t.lineId }, { $set: { quantityReceived: t.prevQty } });
  360:         }
  361:         throw e;
  362:       }
  363:       this.logger.error(`GL post failed for receipt ${receipt.receiptCode}: ${e.message}`, e.stack);
  364:     }
  365: 
  366:     return (await this.listReceipts(b.orderId, organizationId))
  367:       .find(x => String(x._id) === String(receipt._id));
  368:   }
  369: }
