    1: import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
    2: import { PostingService } from '../accounting/posting.service';
    3: import { OrganizationContext } from '../organizations/organization-context';
    4: 
    5: @Injectable()
    6: export class ProcurementGlService {
    7:   private readonly logger = new Logger(ProcurementGlService.name);
    8: 
    9:   constructor(private readonly posting: PostingService) {}
   10: 
   11:   async postReceipt(
   12:     context: OrganizationContext,
   13:     receipt: any,
   14:     receiptLines: any[],
   15:     orderLines: any[],
   16:   ): Promise<void> {
   17:     if (receipt.status === 'rejected') return;
   18: 
   19:     const lineMap = new Map(orderLines.map(l => [String(l._id), l]));
   20:     const total = receiptLines.reduce((s, rl) => {
   21:       const ol = lineMap.get(String(rl.orderLineId));
   22:       return s + (rl.quantityReceived * (ol?.unitPriceUgx ?? 0));
   23:     }, 0);
   24: 
   25:     if (total <= 0) return;
   26: 
   27:     const debitAccount = this.accountForReceiptLines(receiptLines, orderLines);
   28: 
   29:     await this.posting.post(context, {
   30:       date: new Date(receipt.receivedDate),
   31:       description: `Goods receipt: ${receipt.receiptCode}`,
   32:       sourceType: 'GoodsReceipt',
   33:       sourceId: String(receipt._id),
   34:       createdBy: receipt.createdBy || 'system',
   35:       lines: [
   36:         { accountCode: debitAccount, debit: total, memo: `Received ${receiptLines.length} line(s)` },
   37:         { accountCode: '2000', credit: total, memo: `Payable for ${receipt.receiptCode}` },
   38:       ],
   39:     });
   40: 
   41:     this.logger.log(`Posted GL for receipt ${receipt.receiptCode}: ${total} UGX`);
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   private accountForReceiptLines(receiptLines: any[], orderLines: any[]): string {
   45:     const lineMap = new Map(orderLines.map(l => [String(l._id), l]));
   46:     const codes = new Set(receiptLines.map(rl => {
   47:       const ol = lineMap.get(String(rl.orderLineId));
   48:       return this.accountForItem(ol?.itemName ?? '', ol?.itemCode ?? '');
   49:     }));
   50:     if (codes.size === 1) return [...codes][0];
   51:     return '1230';
   52:   }
   53: 
   54:   private accountForItem(itemName: string, itemCode: string): string {
   55:     const t = `${itemName} ${itemCode}`.toLowerCase();
   56:     if (/seedling|sapling|nursery|plant/.test(t)) return '1200';
   57:     if (/honey|jar|bottle/.test(t)) return '1210';
   58:     if (/fuel|diesel|petrol|benzene/.test(t)) return '6210';
   59:     if (/tool|equipment|machinery|chainsaw/.test(t)) return '6200';
   60:     if (/fertilis|fertiliz|chemical|herbicide|pesticide|dung|compost/.test(t)) return '6110';
   61:     return '1230';
   62:   }
   63: }

  118:     if (!task) throw new NotFoundException('Labour task not found.');
  119:     if (!await this.workers.findOne({ organizationId, workerCode: dto.workerCode }).lean())
  120:       throw new NotFoundException('Worker code not found.');
  121: 
  122:     const hours = Number(dto.hoursWorked), output = Number(dto.outputQuantity), cost = Number(dto.labourCostUgx);
  123:     if (![hours, output, cost].every(Number.isFinite) || hours < 0 || output < 0 || cost < 0)
  124:       throw new BadRequestException('Hours, output and labour cost must be non-negative numbers.');
  125: 
  126:     const row: any = await this.entries.create({
  127:       ...dto,
  128:       organizationId,
  129:       hoursWorked: hours,
  130:       outputQuantity: output,
  131:       labourCostUgx: cost,
  132:       recordedBy: user.email,
  133:     });
  134: 
  135:     // Post to GL
  136:     try {
  137:       const context: OrganizationContext = { organizationId, userId: user.email, roles: [] };
  138:       await this.gl.postLabourEntry(context, row.toObject(), task);
  139:     } catch (e: any) {
  140:       if (e?.status === 400 && /locked period/i.test(e.message)) {
  141:         await this.entries.findByIdAndDelete(row._id);
  142:         throw e;
  143:       }
  144:       this.logger.error(`GL post failed for labour entry ${row._id}: ${e.message}`, e.stack);
  145:     }
  146: 
  147:     // Audit AFTER GL so we only log successful end-to-end operations
  148:     await this.audit.record(
  149:       user.email,
  150:       'created',
  151:       'labour_entry',
  152:       String(row._id),
  153:       `Recorded labour output for ${dto.workerCode}`,
  154:       { taskId: dto.taskId, hoursWorked: hours, outputQuantity: output, labourCostUgx: cost },
  155:     );
  156:     return row;
  157:   }
  158: 
  159:   async dashboard(organizationId: string) {
  160:     const [workers, tasks, entries]: any = await Promise.all([
  161:       this.workers.find({ organizationId }).lean(),
  162:       this.tasks.find({ organizationId }).lean(),
  163:       this.entries.find({ organizationId }).lean(),
  164:     ]);
  165:     const byTask = tasks.map((t: any) => {
  166:       const te = entries.filter((e: any) => String(e.taskId) === String(t._id));
  167:       const hours = te.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + Number(e.hoursWorked || 0), 0);
  168:       const output = te.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + Number(e.outputQuantity || 0), 0);
  169:       const costs = te.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + Number(e.labourCostUgx || 0), 0);
  170:       const units = [...new Set(te.map((e: any) => e.outputUnit).filter(Boolean))];
  171:       return {
  172:         ...t, recordedHours: hours, recordedOutput: output, recordedLabourCostUgx: costs,
  173:         outputUnits: units,
  174:         costPerOutputUnit: output > 0 && units.length <= 1 ? costs / output : null,
  175:         entryCount: te.length,
  176:       };
  177:     });
  178:     const totalHours = entries.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + Number(e.hoursWorked || 0), 0);
  179:     const totalOutput = entries.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + Number(e.outputQuantity || 0), 0);
  180:     const totalCost = entries.reduce((s: number, e: any) => s + Number(e.labourCostUgx || 0), 0);
  181:     return {
  182:       generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  183:       totals: {
  184:         workers: workers.length,
  185:         activeWorkers: workers.filter((w: any) => w.status === 'active').length,
  186:         tasks: tasks.length,
  187:         openTasks: tasks.filter((t: any) => !['verified','cancelled'].includes(t.status)).length,
  188:         submittedTasks: tasks.filter((t: any) => t.status === 'submitted').length,
  189:         verifiedTasks: tasks.filter((t: any) => t.status === 'verified').length,
  190:         recordedHours: totalHours,
  191:         recordedOutput: totalOutput,
  192:         recordedLabourCostUgx: totalCost,
  193:         averageCostPerOutputUnit: totalOutput > 0 ? totalCost / totalOutput : null,
  194:       },
  195:       tasks: byTask,
  196:       notes: [
  197:         'Output quantities should only be compared when activity, unit, quality standard and conditions are comparable.',
  198:         'Cost per output unit uses recorded labour cost and recorded output; it is not a full farm production cost.',
  199:         'Labour entries post Dr Expense / Cr Cash on creation.',
  200:         'Worker records contain personal contact information; restrict access and collect only what is necessary.',
  201:       ],
  202:     };
  203:   }

   11:   async postLabourEntry(
   12:     context: OrganizationContext,
   13:     entry: any,
   14:     task: any,
   15:   ): Promise<void> {
   16:     if (!entry.labourCostUgx || entry.labourCostUgx <= 0) return;
   17: 
   18:     const expenseAccount = this.accountForActivity(task?.activity ?? '');
   19: 
   20:     await this.posting.post(context, {
   21:       date: new Date(entry.workDate),
   22:       description: `Labour: ${task?.taskCode ?? 'task'} â€” ${entry.workerCode} (${entry.hoursWorked}h)`,
   23:       sourceType: 'LabourEntry',
   24:       sourceId: String(entry._id),
   25:       createdBy: entry.recordedBy,
   26:       lines: [
   27:         {
   28:           accountCode: expenseAccount,
   29:           debit: entry.labourCostUgx,
   30:           memo: `${task?.activity ?? 'Labour'} â€” ${entry.workerCode}`,
   31:         },
   32:         {
   33:           accountCode: '1000',
   34:           credit: entry.labourCostUgx,
   35:           memo: `Labour payment for ${entry.workerCode}`,
   36:         },
   37:       ],
   38:     });
   39: 
   40:     this.logger.log(
   41:       `Posted labour GL: ${entry.workerCode} ${entry.labourCostUgx} UGX on ${entry.workDate}`,
   42:     );
   43:   }

   45:   private accountForActivity(activity: string): string {
   46:     const a = activity.toLowerCase();
   47:     if (/planting|nursery|seedling|transplant/.test(a)) return '6100';
   48:     if (/hive|bee|apiary|colony/.test(a)) return '6010';
   49:     if (/weed|prun|firebreak|tree count|growth|harvest|site maint|field/.test(a)) return '6000';
   50:     return '6000';
   51:   }
